You are evaluating the complete architecture of the 'Houses of Life' subsystem in Astra,
spanning both the backend mathematical calculus (Hemisphere B) and the frontend presentation layer.

Your mission is to evaluate and judge two critical dimensions:
  DIMENSION A: Mathematical Accuracy & Classical Fidelity
  DIMENSION B: Frontend Information Architecture, Visual Presentation, and Design Protocol

===============================================================================================
DIMENSION A: BACKEND CALCULATION ENGINE & ARCHITECTURE
===============================================================================================
1. WHOLE SIGN GEOMETRY & SENSITIVE CUSPS (Phase 1):
   - Ascendant degree inside its sign defines the sensitive cusp for all 12 houses.
   - Target house H is identified by sign: Sign_H = (AscSignIndex + H - 1) % 12.
   - Cusp longitude: Cusp_H = (Sign_H * 30.0) + AscDegInSign.

2. FUNCTIONAL NATURE RELATIVE TO LAGNA (Phase 2):
   - Based on Parāśara rulership rules: Trikoṇa (1, 5, 9) lords are auspicious benefics;
     Dusthāna (6, 8, 12) and Triṣaḍāya (3, 6, 11) lords are malefics;
     Kendra (1, 4, 7, 10) lords are modified by Kendrādhipati Doṣa.
   - Moolatrikona sign dictates primary nature; secondary Dusthāna rulership is waived if Lord occupies Svakṣetra (15.29).
   - Dynamic temperaments: Sun (-0.5), Moon (scaled by Pakṣa Bala: -0.5 New Moon to +1.0 Full Moon),
     Mercury (conjunction-dependent: +0.5 with benefics, -0.5 with malefics).

3. CONTINUOUS EXACT-DEGREE ASPECTS (*SPHUṬA DṚṢṬI*) & CUSP DOMINANCE (Phase 4):
   - Aspect percentage is derived continuously from exact angular distance via Parāśara's Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi:
     AspectPct = (Virūpas / 60.0) * 100.0.
   - Blind spots (0°-60°, exact 150°, 300°-360°) are calculated organically by the continuous degree curve.
   - No artificial sign-distance masks or hard step thresholds suppress continuous aspects.
   - Multi-planet cusp dominance ('potency stealing') allows closer planets to absorb conjunction intensity.

4. COMPREHENSIVE PLANETARY ASSESSMENT (Phase 5):
   - Multi-varga dignity: 65% D1 + 35% D9 (Vargottama floored at >= 0.80).
   - Directional strength (Digbala): Canonical peak houses with sign-safe Shadbala scaling.
   - Intrinsic Quality (50% Dignity + 35% Aspects + 15% Digbala) is strictly decoupled from Prominence
     (House category & cusp proximity), preventing Kendra cusps from flipping debilitated planets positive.
   - Vilagna protection (Phaladīpikā 3.9) softens negative dignity strain in House 1.
   - Eclipse strain factor (Grahaṇa) attenuates luminaries conjoined with Rahu/Ketu within 12°.

5. DIRECT BASIC HOUSE CONDITIONS (Phase 6):
   - All continuous degree aspects contribute proportionally without arbitrary step cutoffs.
   - Bidirectional natural friendship (P -> Lord and Lord -> P) modifies the functional blend:
     F_eff = F_lagna + FriendMod (eliminating double-counting of inherent temperament).
   - Smooth continuous functional transition across [-0.25, +0.25] without deadband cliff-edges.
   - Condition-gating by Combustion & Bhāva Sandhi (amūḍha-hīnāḥ per 15.1, 15.5), relieved at cusp door (15.14).
   - Multi-varga composite dignity check (d_p < 0.0) so Vargottama / D9-exalted planets are not penalized.
   - Upacaya (3, 6, 11) malefic occupant rule (15.18): strictly for F_eff < 0, scaled positively with dignity.
   - Rahu/Ketu node impact with benefic aspect mitigation and Mars/Saturn malefic aggravation check (15.8).
   - Fixed baseline divisor of 3.0 with hyperbolic tangent saturation: 120.0 * tanh(Total / 360.0),
     guaranteeing strict mathematical monotonicity across all positive and negative rays.

6. RELATIVE HOUSE TECHNIQUE (*BHAVĀT BHAVAM*) & BHĀVA-NĀŚA FIX (Phase 7):
   - Evaluates each target house H as House 1.
   - Lord in relative 6, 8, 12 receives severe Bhāva-nāśa ruination penalty (-70/-110/-80),
     mitigated if dignified/uncombust, waived if occupying Svakṣetra (15.29), and softened by benefic aspects.
   - Relative functional natures are blended 70/30 with natal functional nature, protecting Ascendant Lord (Lagneśa).

7. BHĀVA KĀRAKA & KĀRAKA LAGNA (Phase 7B) AND CHANDRA LAGNA (Phase 7C):
   - Direct condition of natural representative (Kāraka), relative placement, and Kāraka Lagna score.
   - Lunar frame (Chandra Lagna) blended 20% into relative house assessment.

8. HOUSE WALLS / KARTARĪ YOGA (Phase 8) & YOGAS (Phase 9):
   - Quantified flanking within 30° before and after cusp; requires true cross-border adjacency (15.6).
   - Certified Rāja, Daridra, Parivartana, and Mahāpuruṣa yogas saturated via tanh into [-100, +100].

9. COMPOSITE SYNTHESIS & CLASSICAL TIERS (Phase 10):
   - Weighted sum across 6 components: Basic (30%), Lord/Occupants (30%), Relative (15%), Kāraka (10%), Yogas (10%), Walls (5%).
   - Classical Tiers: Puṣṭa (Flourishing: >= +25.0), Miśra (Mixed/Resilient: -25.0 to +25.0), Hīna (Depleted/Afflicted: < -25.0).

KEY VISUAL COMPONENTS IN tmpl_houses_of_life.html & houses_of_life.js:
1. TOP TOOLBAR & VIEW SWITCHER:
   - Title: 'Houses of Life' with micro-tag 'Phaladīpikā Ch. 15'.
   - View Buttons: [12-House Matrix], [Audit Receipt (H1..H12)], [7-Graha Ledger], [Concordance].
   - Maximize Button [⛶]: Expands widget into full-screen dialog.

2. ZONE 1: EXECUTIVE HORIZON RIBBON:
   - Readout Line: Ascendant Anchor degree, Top Flourishing House, Most Challenged House.
   - Diverging Bar Chart Horizon:
     * Scale from -150 to +150 with a centered 0.0 baseline.
     * 12 diverging bars representing each house's composite score.
     * Continuous color interpolation: Forest green for Puṣṭa, warm amber/ochre for Miśra, crimson for Hīna.
     * Interactive click: Clicking any bar immediately activates the Audit Receipt drilldown for that house.

3. VIEW 1: 12-HOUSE MATRIX TABLE:
   - High-density, professional editorial matrix displaying all 12 houses.
   - Columns: House # & Sanskrit Name, Sign & STIX Two Math glyph, Cusp Longitude & Campanus Deg,
     Lord & Dispositor, Occupants & Degree Aspect Rays, Component 1–6 Scores, Composite Score, and Classical Tier.
   - Interactive Row Selection: Highlights active house and opens drilldown receipt.

4. VIEW 2: AUDIT RECEIPT DRILLDOWN:
   - Responsive Dual Mode:
     * In wide cells (>= 720px) or maximized view: Renders side-by-side next to the Matrix table.
     * In compact cells (< 720px): Renders as a dedicated tab with '< Back to Matrix' button and [Prev]/[Next] steppers.
   - 30° Degree Strip SVG:
     * Visualizes the 30° span of the target sign.
     * Renders exact sensitive cusp marker line.
     * Displays physical occupant planet markers and incoming aspect rays relative to the cusp.
   - 6-Component Diagnostic Scorecards:
     * Card 1: Basic House Condition (direct ray contributions table with sign-safe integrity gating).
     * Card 2: Lord & Occupants (Lord final score, Dispositor chain path, effective buffered score).
     * Card 3: Bhavāt Bhavam & Chandra Lagna (Kendra/Trine points, Dusthāna penalty, benefic mitigation %).
     * Card 4: Bhāva Kāraka Assessment (natural significator score, Kāraka Lagna score).
     * Card 5: House Walls / Kartarī Yoga (before/after flanking degrees, wall classification).
     * Card 6: Certified House Yogas (Rāja, Daridra, Parivartana, Mahāpuruṣa active point breakdown).
   - Dispositor Chain Path visualization:
     * Shows traversed houses and final dispositor loop (e.g., Venus -> Moon -> Jupiter -> Venus).

5. VIEW 3: 7-GRAHA OPERATIONAL LEDGER:
   - Standalone planetary assessment table for Sun through Saturn.
   - Columns: Graha, Tropical Sign, House, Multi-Varga Dignity (D1+D9), Aspects Net, Digbala Points,
     Quality Base, Cusp Proximity Factor, House Multiplier, Combustion Loss, Bhāva Sandhi, Eclipse Loss, Final Score.

6. VIEW 4: STAGE 3 VS. STAGE 4 CONCORDANCE:
   - Cross-checks Stage 3 qualitative house strengths against Stage 4 quantitative Houses of Life scores,
     identifying reinforcing strengths or divergence.

7. STRICT ASTRA EDITORIAL DESIGN SYSTEM COMPLIANCE:
   - Strict 12px Font Floor (Rule 1 of static/GEMINI.md): Zero sub-pixel font sizes.
   - Monochrome STIX Two Math Line Glyphs (\uFE0E): Eliminates colored OS emoji boxes for signs/planets.
   - Anti-Pill Protocol: Routine data (degrees, coordinates, dispositors) rendered as clean tabular numbers.
   - Zero Consumer Emojis: Replaced by semantic CSS micro-dots (.status-indicator) and square micro-tags (.micro-tag).

# Houses of Life (Vic DiCara / Phaladīpikā Chapter 15 Evaluation System)

## 1. Overview & Foundational Mathematical Architecture

The **Houses of Life** system is an advanced quantitative astrological engine developed by Vic DiCara, rooted in Chapters 3, 4, 6, and 15 of Mantreśvara's classical authority *Phaladīpikā*. It provides a rigorous, multi-stage evaluation of the 12 houses (*Bhāvas*) and 7 classical physical planets (*Grahas*): Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, and Saturn, alongside the nodal axes (Rāhu and Ketu).

Unlike qualitative traditions that rely on broad rules of thumb, Houses of Life computes continuous mathematical scores across distinct phases without rounding intermediate arc-minutes or arc-seconds:

$$\text{Composite House Score}_H = 0.30\,\text{Basic}_H + 0.30\,\text{Lord}_H + 0.15\,\text{Relative}_H + 0.10\,\text{Kāraka}_H + 0.10\,\text{Yoga}_H + 0.05\,\text{Wall}_H$$

*(Note: When a house is unwalled ($\text{is\_walled} = \text{False}$), the $5\%$ wall weight is redistributed proportionally across the remaining active components by dividing by $0.95$).*

---

## 2. Phase-by-Phase Computational Workflow

### Phase 1: Chart Geometry & Sensitive House Cusps

1. **Whole Sign House Framework (*Rāśi Bhāva*):**
   * The zodiac sign containing the Ascendant (*Lagna*) forms the 1st House.
   * Subsequent signs in natural zodiacal order constitute Houses 2 through 12.
   * Each house encompasses a complete $30^\circ$ sign boundary.

2. **Sensitive Cusp Degree ("The Front Door"):**
   * The exact degree of the Ascendant within its sign ($\lambda_{\text{Asc}} \pmod{30^\circ}$) establishes the sensitive cusp center for all 12 houses.
   * **Cusp Longitude Formula:**
     $$\text{Cusp}_H = \left( (S_{\text{Asc}} + H - 1) \times 30^\circ + (\lambda_{\text{Asc}} \pmod{30^\circ}) \right) \pmod{360^\circ}$$
     where $S_{\text{Asc}} \in \{0, \dots, 11\}$ is the 0-indexed sign of the Ascendant, and $H \in \{1, \dots, 12\}$.

---

### Phase 2: Planetary Functional Nature Quantification ($F_P$)

Derived from *Phaladīpikā* Chapter 15 and *BPHS* Chapter 34, the functional nature of each planet relative to the Ascendant is quantified via sequential rules with *Yogakāraka*, *Kendrādhipatya*, and *Svakṣetra* adjustments:

#### 1. Mūlatrikoṇa (Root) vs. Regular Domicile Definitions
* Dual-ruling planets assign the **male (odd) sign** as Mūlatrikoṇa root and the **female (even) sign** as regular domicile.
* **Sun:** Leo (male Mūlatrikoṇa).
* **Exception 1 (Moon):** As the Moon owns no male sign, its exaltation sign **Taurus** acts as its Mūlatrikoṇa root, while **Cancer** is its regular domicile.
* **Exception 2 (Mercury):** Because Mercury derives higher strength from femininity, **Virgo** (female) serves as its Mūlatrikoṇa root, while **Gemini** (male) is regular domicile.
* **Mars:** Aries (Mūlatrikoṇa), Scorpio (Domicile).
* **Jupiter:** Sagittarius (Mūlatrikoṇa), Pisces (Domicile).
* **Venus:** Libra (Mūlatrikoṇa), Taurus (Domicile).
* **Saturn:** Aquarius (Mūlatrikoṇa), Capricorn (Domicile).

#### 2. The Four Sequential Rules
* **Rule 1 (1st House Lord):**
  If planet $P$ rules House 1, assign $F_P = +3$ and **STOP** (skip Rules 2, 3, and 4).
  * *Unblemished Sovereign:* Even if the 1st lord is a natural malefic or simultaneously rules a Dusthāna (e.g., Mars for Aries Lagna ruling 8th Scorpio, or Saturn for Capricorn Lagna), it receives a flat $+3$.
* **Rule 2 (Trine Lords — Houses 5 and 9):**
  * Mūlatrikoṇa sign on House 5 or 9: $+2$.
  * Regular domicile sign on House 5 or 9: $+1$.
* **Rule 3 (Dusthāna Lords — Houses 6, 8, and 12):**
  * Mūlatrikoṇa sign on House 6, 8, or 12: $-2$.
  * Regular domicile sign on House 6, 8, or 12: $-1$.
  * *Simultaneous Trine & Dusthāna Ownership:* Scores are summed side-by-side (e.g., Jupiter for Aries Lagna gets $+2$ for H9 Sagittarius and $-1$ for H12 Pisces).
  * *Moon Dual-Dusthāna Exception (Sagittarius Ascendant):* When Taurus is House 6 and Cancer is House 8, the Mūlatrikoṇa root and domicile are evaluated as an **OR** condition rather than cumulative, assigning Moon $-2$ (not $-3$).
  * *Svakṣetra Dual-Lordship Override (Phaladīpikā 15.29):* When planet $P$ physically occupies one of its own signs ($P_{\text{sign}} \in \{\text{MT}, \text{Dom}\}$) corresponding to a non-Dusthāna house ($H \notin \{6, 8, 12\}$), its secondary Dusthāna score is set to $0$ (`dusthana_score = 0`).
* **Rule 4 (Inherent Natural Temperament & BPHS Ch. 34 Adjustments):**
  * Natural benefics: `Jupiter` ($+1.0$), `Venus` ($+1.0$).
  * Natural malefics: `Mars` ($-1.0$), `Saturn` ($-1.0$).
  * `Sun`: $-0.5$ (mild *Krūra* / natural malefic so it properly triggers Upacaya rules in $3, 6, 11$).
  * `Moon`: Evaluated via *Pakṣa Bala* (brightness elongation $\Delta_{\text{Moon-Sun}} \in [0^\circ, 180^\circ]$ per *Phaladīpikā* Ch. 4 & 15.1):
    $$\text{PakṣaFactor} = \frac{\Delta_{\text{Moon-Sun}}}{180.0} \in [0.0, 1.0], \qquad \text{Inherent}_{\text{Moon}} = -0.5 + 1.5 \times \text{PakṣaFactor}$$
    (ranges from $-0.5$ at New Moon to $+1.0$ at Full Moon; defaults to $+0.5$ if positions are absent).
  * `Mercury`: Defaults to $+0.5$ (natural benefic), unless within $10^\circ$ of a natural malefic (`Mars`, `Saturn`, `Rahu`, `Ketu`) in the same sign, in which case it assumes $-0.5$.
  * **Supreme Rāja Yogakāraka Bonus (*BPHS* 34.40–43):** If a planet simultaneously owns a Kendra ($4, 7, 10$) and a Trine ($5, 9$) (e.g., Mars for Cancer/Leo Lagna, Saturn for Taurus/Libra Lagna), its natural maleficence is waived and it receives $+1$ (`inherent_score = max(0, inherent_score) + 1`).
  * **Māraka & Triṣaḍāya Preservation:** Natural malefics owning a Kendra without a Trine retain their inherent maleficence ($-1.0$).

#### 3. Net Functional Classification
$$F_P = \begin{cases} +3 & \text{if } P \text{ rules House 1} \\ \text{Score}_{\text{Trine}} + \text{Score}_{\text{Dusthāna}} + \text{Score}_{\text{Inherent}} & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}$$
* $F_P > 0 \implies$ Functionally Benefic.
* $F_P < 0 \implies$ Functionally Malefic.
* $F_P = 0 \implies$ Functionally Neutral.

---

### Phase 3: Rāśi Dignity Scale ($d_P$) & Universal Scaling Law

#### 1. D1 Rāśi Dignity Scale ($d_P \in [-1.0, +1.0]$, $D_{\text{pct}} \in [-100, +100]$)
* **Compound Friendship (*Pañcadhā Maitrī*):** Evaluates planetary dignity using the classical 5-Fold Compound Relationship (`dignity_breakdown["final_dignity"]`), blending eternal Natural Friendship (*Naisargika Sambandha*) with chart-specific Temporary Friendship (*Tātkālika Sambandha* based on 3-sign separation) to determine how comfortable the planet feels in its host sign:
  * **Exaltation (*Uccha*):** $+1.0$ ($+100\%$)
  * **Mūlatrikoṇa:** $+0.9$ ($+90\%$)
  * **Domicile (*Svakṣetra*):** $+0.8$ ($+80\%$)
  * **Very Friendly (*Adhi Mitra*):** $+0.6$ ($+60\%$)
  * **Friendly (*Mitra*):** $+0.4$ ($+40\%$)
  * **Neutral (*Sama*):** $0.0$ ($0\%$)
  * **Inimical (*Śatru*):** $-0.4$ ($-40\%$)
  * **Very Inimical (*Adhi Śatru*):** $-0.7$ ($-70\%$)
  * **Debilitated (*Nīca*):** $-1.0$ ($-100\%$)

#### 2. Universal Dignity-Scaling Law (`scale_by_dignity`)
Scales any base impact $B \ge 0$ by dignity $d_P$ according to functional nature $F$:
* **Functionally Positive ($F \ge 0.25$):**
  * $d_P \ge 0 \implies S = B \times (1.0 + d_P)$ (Up to $2\times$ intensification).
  * $d_P < 0 \implies S = B \times (1.0 - 0.80 \times |d_P|)$ (Severe debilitation retains only $0.20\times$ good results per *Phaladīpikā* 15.18).
* **Functionally Negative ($F \le -0.25$):**
  * $d_P \le 0 \implies S = B \times (1.0 + |d_P|)$ (Bad mood intensifies malice up to $2\times$).
  * $d_P > 0 \implies S = B \times (1.0 - \frac{d_P}{2.0})$ (Good mood mitigates malice by up to half).
* **Continuous Functional Transition ($-0.25 < F < 0.25$):**
  * Continuous linear blend anchored at neutral $S = B \times d_P$ at $F = 0$, eliminating cliff edges and abrupt sign inversions.

---

### Phase 4: Conjunctions, Cross-Border Halving & Cusp Dominance

1. **Continuous Exact-Degree Aspects (*Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi*):**
   * Derived continuously from exact angular distance $\Delta\theta = (\lambda_{\text{cusp}} - \lambda_{\text{planet}}) \pmod{360^\circ}$ via Parāśara's exact *Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi* formulation (`aspects.get_graha_drishti`), expressed on a $0\%$ to $100\%$ scale: $\frac{\text{Virūpas}}{60.0} \times 100.0$.
   * Natural classical blind spots ($0^\circ\text{–}60^\circ$, exact $150^\circ$, and $300^\circ\text{–}360^\circ$) are evaluated organically through the continuous degree calculus, perfectly matching the *Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi* aspect matrix for sensitive Whole Sign cusps.
2. **Conjunction Proximity:**
   * Within $30^\circ$: $C_{\text{raw}} = \max\left(0.0, \left(1.0 - \frac{\Delta\lambda}{30.0}\right) \times 100.0\right)$.
3. **Cross-Border Attenuation:**
   * If a conjunction spans across a sign boundary, potency is halved ($\times 0.5$).
4. **Multi-Planet Cusp Dominance ("Potency Stealing"):**
   * Co-occupants of the same Whole Sign house share cusp alignment. In multi-planet stelliums ($\ge 2$ co-occupants), planets are sorted descending by their initial cusp proximity ($C_{\text{raw}}$).
   * Across all pairs, any closer co-occupant $P_{\text{near}}$ absorbs conjunction points from a farther co-occupant $P_{\text{far}}$ when $C_{\text{raw}, \text{near}} > C_{\text{raw}, \text{far}}$ and their separation is within $15^\circ$:
     $$\Delta_{\text{steal}} = C_{\text{far}} \times \frac{C_{\text{near}}}{C_{\text{near}} + C_{\text{far}}} \times \left(1.0 - \frac{\Delta\theta_{P_{\text{near}}, P_{\text{far}}}}{15^\circ}\right)$$
     $$C_{\text{near, final}} = \min(125.0, C_{\text{near}} + \Delta_{\text{steal}}), \qquad C_{\text{far, final}} = \max(0.0, C_{\text{far}} - \Delta_{\text{steal}})$$
   * Equidistant co-occupants retain their respective conjunctions without arbitrary asymmetric theft, and pairwise processing ensures order-independence across 3+ planet stelliums.
5. **Mutual Sign Exchange Connection (*Mitho Yoga / Parivartana*, *Phaladīpikā* 15.30):**
   * Two planets in mutual reception / sign exchange act as connected (*yukta* / *bandha*), receiving a $50.0\%$ mutual influence floor in `planet_to_planet` ($\max(\text{AspectPct}, \text{ConjPct}, 50.0\%)$) even when separated by disparate signs without physical Graha Dṛṣṭi.

---

### Phase 5: Comprehensive Planetary Assessment (Quality vs. Prominence)

1. **Multi-Varga Dignity Blending ($d_P$):**
   * Incorporates D1 and D9 (*Navāṃśa*, *Phaladīpikā* 3.4 & 3.11).
   * **Vargottama** ($S_{\text{D1}} == S_{\text{D9}}$): Effective dignity is floored at $\max(d_{\text{D1}}, 0.80)$ (acting as own sign).
   * Otherwise: $d_P = 0.65 \times d_{\text{D1}} + 0.35 \times d_{\text{D9}}$.
   * *Active across all house phases:* This composite dignity unit $d_P$ is passed into and utilized across Phase 6 (Basic House Conditions), Phase 7 (Relative Houses), and Phase 8 (House Walls).
2. **Directional Strength (*Digbala*, *Phaladīpikā* Ch. 4):**
   * Canonical directional strength based on peak house $H_{\text{peak}}$ (Jupiter/Mercury in H1, Sun/Mars in H10, Saturn in H7, Moon/Venus in H4):
     $$\text{DigFactor}_P = 1.0 - \frac{\text{shortest\_house\_dist}(H_P, H_{\text{peak}})}{3.0} \in [-1.0, +1.0]$$
     $$\text{BaseDigBala}_P = 20.0 \times \text{DigFactor}_P$$
   * **Sign-Safe Shadbala Scaling:** Clamped within $[0.75, 1.25]$:
     * If $\text{BaseDigBala}_P \ge 0$: $\text{DigBalaPts}_P = \text{BaseDigBala}_P \times \text{ClampedBala} + (\text{ClampedBala} - 1.0) \times 20.0$
     * If $\text{BaseDigBala}_P < 0$: $\text{DigBalaPts}_P = \frac{\text{BaseDigBala}_P}{\text{ClampedBala}} + (\text{ClampedBala} - 1.0) \times 20.0$
3. **Intrinsic Quality ($\text{QualityBase}_P$):**
   * Combines Dignity, Incoming Aspects, and Digbala without raw cusp proximity additions:
     $$\text{QualityBase}_P = 0.50 \times D_{\text{pct}, P} + 0.35 \times \text{Inf}_P + 0.15 \times \text{DigBalaPts}_P$$
4. **Incoming Influences & Nodal Afflictions / Eclipses (*Grahaṇa*):**
   * **Incoming Planetary Rays:** When $|F_Q| \le 0.15$, signed impact adopts $S = B \times d_Q$ directly without double multiplication.
   * When Rāhu or Ketu is in the same sign within $15.0^\circ$ of planet $P$:
     * Conjunction intensity: $\text{node\_conj} = \left(1.0 - \frac{\Delta\lambda_{\text{node}}}{15.0}\right) \times 100.0$.
     * **Luminaries (Sun, Moon):** Subtract $0.85 \times \text{node\_conj}$. An eclipse strain factor $\text{eclipse\_loss} = 0.15 \times \left(1.0 - \frac{\Delta\lambda_{\text{node}}}{15.0}\right)$ attenuates intrinsic quality when $\Delta\lambda \le 12.0^\circ$. For the Sun placed in an Upacaya house ($3, 6, 11$) with composite dignity $d_P \ge 0.4$, both $\text{node\_pen}$ and $\text{eclipse\_loss}$ are halved.
     * **Other Planets:** Subtract $0.50 \times \text{node\_conj}$ (halved in Upacayas $3, 6, 11$).
   * **Dusthāna Enemy Conjunction Attenuation:** Conjunctions with a Dusthāna lord ($6, 8, 12$) who is a natural enemy are attenuated by $0.50\times$.
5. **House Placement & Cusp Proximity Prominence Multiplier:**
   * Prominent houses ($1, 4, 5, 9, 10$): Base $1.25\times$.
   * Regular houses ($2, 7, 11$): Base $1.00\times$.
   * Hidden houses ($3, 6, 8, 12$): Base $0.75\times$ to $1.00\times$.
   * **Afflicted Dusthāna Lord Penalty:** If a lord of $6, 8, 12$ sits in a Dusthāna with $d_{\text{D1}} < 0$, it receives a $0.70\times$ placement penalty.
   * **Cusp Proximity Factor:**
     $$\text{CuspProxFactor}_P = 0.80 + 0.40 \times \left(\frac{\text{CappedBase}_P}{100.0}\right)$$
     $$\text{EffectiveMultiplier}_P = \text{BaseMultiplier}_P \times \text{CuspProxFactor}_P$$
   * **Vilagna Protection (*Phaladīpikā* 3.9):** If a planet occupies House 1 with $\text{QualityBase} < 0$, its negative dignity strain is softened by $0.50 \times \text{CuspProxFactor}$.
6. **Bhāva Sandhi & Cusp Proximity Relief (*Phaladīpikā* 15.14 & 8.34–35):**
   * A planet within $4.0^\circ$ of a sign border faces junction weakness: $\text{RawSandhiPen} = \max(0.0, 1.0 - \frac{\Delta\lambda_{\text{border}}}{4.0^\circ})$.
   * Per *Phaladīpikā* 15.14 (*bhāveṣu bhāvasphuṭatulyabhāgas tadbhāvajaṃ pūrṇaphalaṃ vidhatte*) and 8.34–35:
     * If the sensitive house cusp itself is near the border ($< 4.0^\circ$) and the planet is inside the house between the cusp and sign center ($\Delta\lambda_{\text{border}} \ge \Delta\lambda_{\text{cusp, border}}$), or if the planet is tightly conjunct the cusp ($\Delta\lambda_{\text{cusp}} \le 5.0^\circ$), the planet is situated directly at the cusp door inside the house and is completely relieved of sandhi penalty ($\text{CuspRelief} = 0.0, \text{SandhiPenalty} = 0.0$).
     * For distances $5.0^\circ < \Delta\lambda_{\text{cusp}} < 10.0^\circ$, relief scales smoothly via linear interpolation:
       $$\text{CuspRelief} = \frac{\Delta\lambda_{\text{cusp}} - 5.0^\circ}{5.0^\circ}$$
     * If $\Delta\lambda_{\text{cusp}} \ge 10.0^\circ$, full sandhi penalty applies ($\text{CuspRelief} = 1.0$).
     * Final penalty: $\text{SandhiPenalty} = \text{RawSandhiPen} \times \text{CuspRelief}$.
7. **Combustion & Solar Gain:** Proximity to Sun within combustion orb.
8. **Sign-Safe Synthesis:** Produces `final_assessment_score`.

---

### Phase 6: Basic House Condition — Direct Evaluation

For each house $H \in \{1, \dots, 12\}$:
1. Impact $I_{P \to H} = \max(\text{AspectPct}_{P \to \text{Cusp}_H}, \text{DominanceConjPct}_{P \to H})$.
   * All continuous aspects contribute directly and proportionally without artificial step cutoffs.
2. Dignity $d_P$ and Effective Functional Nature $F_{P, H}$:
   * **House Lord:** $F_{\text{eff}} = 2.0$.
   * **Non-Lord Planets:**
     * **Exaltation / Mūlatrikoṇa / Domicile Sign Override:** If the target house sign $H$ is planet $P$'s Exaltation, Mūlatrikoṇa root, or regular Domicile sign (e.g., Moon on Taurus $H=10$ for Leo Lagna), and the planet is non-malefic ($\text{InherentScore} \ge 0.0$ or $F_{\text{lagna}} \ge 0.0$), the planet intrinsically harmonizes with and enriches the sign itself, setting $\text{FriendMod} = +0.50$ regardless of interpersonal friction with the house lord.
     * **Natural Friendship Matrix (*Naisargika Sambandha*, *Phaladīpikā* 15.5 & 15.7):** For other signs, bidirectional relationship ($P \to \text{Lord}$ and $\text{Lord} \to P$) modifies the baseline:
       * If both are Friends: $\text{FriendMod} = +0.50$
       * If either considers the other an Enemy: $\text{FriendMod} = -0.50$
       * If one is Friend and the other Neutral: $\text{FriendMod} = +0.25$
       * Otherwise: $\text{FriendMod} = 0.0$
     * **Effective Functional Formula:**
       $$F_{\text{eff}} = 0.50 \times F_{\text{lagna}} + 0.50 \times \text{InherentScore} + 0.35 \times \text{FriendMod}$$
       *(Scales $F_{\text{lagna}}$ by $0.50$ so non-lords remain bounded on the $[-2.0, +2.0]$ scale below or equal to the House Lord $2.00$, preserving Rāja Yogakāraka status and natural benefic cushions while factoring archetypal relationship with the house lord).*
3. **Continuous Signed Ray Impact Synthesis (`_compute_signed_ray_impact`):**
   * Eliminates deadband cliffs and sign inversions across the functional transition $[-0.25, +0.25]$:
     * If $F_{\text{eff}} \ge 0.25$: $\text{signed\_impact} = \text{scale\_by\_dignity}(I, d_P, 1.0) \times (1.0 + \text{slope} \times (F_{\text{eff}} - 1.0))$
     * If $F_{\text{eff}} \le -0.25$: $\text{signed\_impact} = \text{scale\_by\_dignity}(I, d_P, -1.0) \times -(1.0 + \text{slope} \times (|F_{\text{eff}}| - 1.0))$
     * If $-0.25 < F_{\text{eff}} < 0.25$: continuous linear blend anchored at neutral $I \times d_P$.
   * Utilized consistently in Phase 5 ($\text{slope} = 0.20$ for planet-to-planet aspects) and Phase 6 ($\text{slope} = 0.65$ for cusp rays).
4. **Upacaya (3, 6, 11) Malefic Occupant Rule (*Phaladīpikā* 15.18):**
   * Triggered **strictly when functioning as a malefic ($F_{\text{eff}} < 0$)**. Benefic 1st House Lords and Rāja Yogakārakas are never slashed by friendly fire.
   * Scaled **positively** by dignity (exalted gives strong resilience; debilitated gives weak resilience):
     $$\text{Impact}_{P \to H} = \text{scale\_by\_dignity}(I, d_P, 1.0) \times 0.35$$
5. **Condition-Gating (*Amūḍha-Hīnāḥ*, *Phaladīpikā* 15.1, 15.5):**
   $$\text{ConditionIntegrity} = (1.0 - 0.75 \times \text{CombLoss}_P) \times (1.0 - 0.50 \times \text{SandhiPen}_P)$$
   * Benefic impacts ($\text{signed\_impact} > 0$) are multiplied by $\text{ConditionIntegrity}$.
   * **Multi-Varga Negative Dignity Attenuation (*Arinīca-Mūḍha*):** Evaluated against composite multi-varga dignity ($d_P < 0$). If $d_P < 0$, positive rays are attenuated smoothly: $\text{ConditionIntegrity} \times (1.0 - 0.40 \times |d_P|)$, and by an additional $0.75\times$ if occupying a Dusthāna ($6, 8, 12$). Planets rescued by D9 or Vargottama ($d_P \ge 0$) are not penalized.
   * Malefic impacts ($\text{signed\_impact} < 0$) are intensified by combustion: $(1.0 + 0.35 \times \text{CombLoss}_P) \times (1.0 - 0.30 \times \text{SandhiPen}_P)$.
6. **Rāhu & Ketu Cusp Impact (*Phaladīpikā* 15.8):**
   * Upacaya houses ($3, 6, 11$): $+0.35 \times C_{\text{node}}$.
   * Non-Upacaya houses: $-0.60 \times C_{\text{node}} \times \text{MitFactor}$ (where $\text{MitFactor} = 0.50$ if node is dignified or aspected/conjoined by Jupiter or Venus; $1.25$ if aggravated by Mars/Saturn; $1.00$ otherwise).
7. **Strict Monotonicity & Cusp Score Normalization:**
   * Fixed baseline divisor ($4.0$) matching the natural 7-planet continuous aspect density with hyperbolic tangent saturation:
     $$\text{RawHouseScore}_H = \frac{\sum \text{SignedImpact}}{4.0}$$
     $$\text{BoundedScore}_H = 120.0 \times \tanh\left(\frac{\text{RawHouseScore}_H}{120.0}\right)$$
   * Guaranteed monotonicity: every additional benefic aspect strictly increases the house score, and every additional malefic aspect strictly decreases it.
8. **Spreadsheet-Grade Calculation Ledger (`calculation_ledger`):**
   * Serializes full tabular disclosure for all 12 houses across 10 analytical columns:
     1. `Graha`: Monochrome glyph (`\uFE0E`), planet name, and role (`Lord`, `Occupant`, `Aspect`, `Node`).
     2. `Pos & ΔCusp`: D1 sign, degree in sign, and shortest angular distance ($\Delta\theta^\circ$) to $\text{Cusp}_H$.
     3. `Aspect %`: Exact Parāśara *Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi* onto $\text{Cusp}_H$: $(\text{Virūpas}/60) \times 100\%$.
     4. `Conj %`: $30^\circ$ linear conjunction proximity ($0.5\times$ if cross-border) $\to$ after cusp dominance stealing.
     5. `Base Ray (I)`: $I_{P \to H} = \max(\text{Aspect\%}, \text{Conj}_{\text{dom}}\%)$.
     6. `Func (F_eff)`: Effective functional nature: $2.00$ for Lord; else $0.50\,F_{\text{lagna}} + 0.50\,\text{Inh} + 0.35\,\text{FriendMod}$.
     7. `Dignity (d_P)`: Composite D1+D9 dignity unit $d_P \in [-1.00, +1.00]$ (or Vargottama $\ge +0.80$).
     8. `Scaled Ray`: Ray after `scale_by_dignity` and functional multiplier $f_{\text{mult}}$ (or $+0.35\times$ Upacaya malefic boost).
     9. `Gate Mult`: *Amūḍha-hīnāḥ* integrity multiplier from Combustion, Sandhi, and negative $d_P$.
     10. `Net Ray`: Exact signed points contributed by this row ($\text{Scaled Ray} \times \text{Gate Mult}$).
   * **9 Canonical Graha/Node Rows:** Always ordered `Sun`, `Moon`, `Mars`, `Mercury`, `Jupiter`, `Venus`, `Saturn`, `Rahu`, `Ketu`.
   * **3 Summary Footer Proofs:**
     1. $\sum \text{Raw Signed Cusp Rays}$ ($\text{raw\_total\_signed}$)
     2. $\div \text{Fixed Baseline Divisor } 4.0$ ($\text{raw\_divided\_score}$)
     3. $\text{Final Basic Score } (120 \times \tanh(\text{Raw} / 120))$ (matching the `Basic (30%)` column in the 12-House Matrix).

---

### Phase 7: Relative House Technique — *Bhavāt Bhavam* (*Phaladīpikā* 15.2–15.5)

Treats each target house $H$ as House 1:
1. **Functional Nature Blending:**
   * Relative functional nature from sign $H$ is blended with the native's natal functional nature:
     $$F_{\text{rel}} = 0.70 \times F_{\text{relative}} + 0.30 \times F_{\text{natal}}$$
   * The native's Ascendant Lord (Lagneśa) is protected across all relative house examinations: $\max(0.50, F_{\text{rel}})$.
2. **House Lord Relative Placement ($R_{\text{Lord}, H}$):**
   * **Relative 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10 (*Kendra / Trikoṇa / 2nd*, 15.2):** Weight $1.0$, plus degree-aspect proximity bonus when close to exact $0^\circ, 90^\circ, 120^\circ, 180^\circ$ aspect angles.
   * **Relative 3, 11 (*Upacaya*, 15.18):** Weight $0.50$.
   * **Relative 6, 8, 12 (*Bhāva-Nāśa*, 15.3 & 15.5):**
     * If the Lord is in its other own sign (*Svakṣetra* per 15.29), penalty is $0.0$.
     * Otherwise, base penalty: $-110.0$ in R8, $-80.0$ in R12, $-70.0$ in R6.
     * Halved ($0.50\times$) if the Lord is dignified ($d_P \ge 0.4$) and uncombust; amplified if debilitated or combust.
     * Benefic aspects onto the Lord mitigate this penalty.
3. **Non-Lord Planets in Relative Houses:**
   * Relative 2, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10: Weight $1.0$.
   * Relative 11: Weight $0.50$; Relative 3: Weight $0.40$ (secondary Upacaya growth support).
   * Relative 1: $+0.50\times$ if $F_{\text{rel}} > 0$; $-0.75\times$ if $F_{\text{rel}} < 0$.
   * Relative 6, 8, 12: Adds to raw Dusthāna penalty if $F_{\text{rel}} < 0$.
4. **Benefic Mitigation:** Malefics in relative Dusthānas are mitigated strictly by direct aspects from relative benefics.
5. **Denominator Floor:** $\text{denom} = \max(3.0, N_{\text{active}})$.

---

### Phase 7B: Bhāva Kāraka & Kāraka Lagna Engine (*Phaladīpikā* 15.5–6, 15.15–20, 15.25)

Every house evaluation incorporates the condition and perspective of its natural representative (*Bhāva Kāraka*):
* **Kāraka Directory:**
  * House 1: Sun | House 2: Jupiter | House 3: Mars
  * House 4: Moon, Mercury | House 5: Jupiter | House 6: Saturn, Mars
  * House 7: Venus | House 8: Saturn | House 9: Jupiter, Sun
  * House 10: Mercury, Sun, Jupiter, Saturn | House 11: Jupiter | House 12: Saturn
* **Three-Factor Synthesis for each Kāraka $K$:**
  1. Direct Planet Condition ($\text{k\_planet\_score} = \text{planets\_eval}[K]$).
  2. Relative House Placement & Proximity ($R_{K, H} = ((\text{House}_K - H) \pmod{12}) + 1$, *Phaladīpikā* 15.25):
     * Kendra / Trine / 2nd / 11th: $+15.0$ (`"Close / Nourishing"`)
     * Dusthāna ($6, 8, 12$): $-15.0$ (`"Distant / Dusthāna"`)
     * Upacaya 3rd: $+5.0$ (`"Moderate / Upacaya"`)
     * *Phaladīpikā 15.25 Distinction:* Distinguishes relationship proximity/closeness (`karaka_relative_houses`, `karaka_proximity`) from inner happiness (*Saukhya*).
  3. Kāraka Lagna Score ($\text{k\_lagna\_house\_score}$): Comprehensive Sign-Level Baseline condition of the natal house corresponding to the $H$-th sign counted forward from Kāraka $K$:
     $$\text{SignBaseline}_H = 0.50 \times \text{Basic}_H + 0.30 \times \text{LordAndOccAvg}_H + 0.20 \times \text{Relative}_H$$
  $$\text{Score}_K = 0.55 \times \text{k\_planet\_score} + 0.30 \times \text{k\_lagna\_house\_score} + 0.15 \times \text{k\_rel\_bonus}$$

---

### Phase 7C: Chandra Lagna (Lunar Reference Frame) Assessment

* Computes the corresponding house counted from the Moon's occupied house:
  $$H_{\text{Chandra}} = ((H_{\text{Moon}} - 1 + H - 1) \pmod{12}) + 1$$
* Evaluates the lunar perspective utilizing the comprehensive Sign-Level Baseline $\text{SignBaseline}_{H_{\text{Chandra}}}$ (Cusp + Lord + Relative placement) rather than direct cusp rays alone.
* Exposed in `components["chandra_lagna_assessment"]` with baseline score from $H_{\text{Chandra}}$.
* **Active Mathematical Weight:** Blended directly with Relative Houses (*Bhavāt Bhavam*) before computing the house composite:
  $$R_{\text{combined}, H} = 0.80 \times \text{Relative}_H + 0.20 \times \text{Chandra}_H$$

---

### Phase 8: Quantified House Walls — *Kartarī Yoga*

* Evaluates physical planets within $(0^\circ, 30^\circ)$ before and after $\text{Cusp}_H$.
* **Symmetric Cross-Border Kartarī Sign Adjacency (*Phaladīpikā* 15.6 & DiCara Ep. 4):**
  Requires true flanking across sign borders:
  * $P_{\text{before}}$ within $30^\circ$ before the cusp must sit in $\text{Sign}_{H-1}$ or $\text{Sign}_H$.
  * $P_{\text{after}}$ within $30^\circ$ after the cusp must sit in $\text{Sign}_H$ or $\text{Sign}_{H+1}$.
  * Both cannot sit exclusively in $\text{Sign}_{H-1}$ or exclusively in $\text{Sign}_{H+1}$.
  * This fully supports late Ascendants/cusps ($15^\circ\text{–}30^\circ$) where $P_{\text{before}}$ sits in $\text{Sign}_H$ and $P_{\text{after}}$ sits in $\text{Sign}_{H+1}$.
* Flanking planets occupying the same Whole Sign house are attenuated by $0.50$.
* Compressed into $[-120, +120]$ range via hyperbolic tangent:
  $$\text{WallScore}_H = 120.0 \times \tanh\left(\frac{\text{RawScore}_H}{180.0}\right)$$

---

### Phase 9: Numerical House Yoga Scoring (Certified Classical Yoga Engine)

* Ingests certified classical yogas directly from Astra's `jyotish/yogas/` engine (`chart_dict["yogas"]`).
* **Classical Affliction Detection:** Expands `is_negative_yoga` to catch all classical affliction, poverty, disgrace, and friction patterns (`Niḥsva`, `Duṣkṛti`, `Mṛti`, `Śatru`, `Adhama`, `Aśubha`, `Depleted`, `Disgraced`, `Broken Initiatives`, `Pāpakartarī`, `Bandhana`, `Apakīrti`, `Dusthāna`, `Inauspicious`, etc.).
* **Global Nabhasa Sankhya Restriction:** Broad pattern yogas formed by $\ge 6$ planets (e.g., `Dāma`, `Pāśa`, `Kedāra`, `Śūla`, `Yuga`, `Gola`, `Vallakī`) do NOT credit all 12 owned houses; instead, their impact is focused strictly on physically occupied houses with $0.35\times$ base points.
* **Direct Attribution (Anti-Smearing):** Credit is awarded strictly to:
  * **Occupied Houses:** $1.00\times$ where yoga-forming planets physically sit.
  * **Explicit Participating Houses:** $0.75\times$ (or directly owned houses at $0.50\times$).
* **Rescaled Base Points:**
  * Parivartana: $65.0$
  * Mahāpuruṣa: $65.0$
  * Major Rāja / Dhana: $60.0$
  * Viparīta Rāja: $65.0$ (awards full points to Dusthāna houses $6, 8, 12$ per *Phaladīpikā* 6.57–70)
  * Minor Auspicious: $30.0$
  * Negative / Affliction: $-60.0$
* **Smooth Saturation:**
  $$\text{TotalYogaScore}_H = 100.0 \times \tanh\left(\frac{Y_{\text{raw}, H}}{120.0}\right)$$

---

### Phase 10: Dispositor Chain Tracking & House Lord Synthesis

#### 1. The "Second-Time Tracked = Final Dispositor" Rule
Traverses planet $\to$ sign lord until a planet is encountered a second time.

#### 2. Dispositor Synthesis Archetypes
1. **Svakṣetra Lord (1-hop):** $\text{EffectiveLordScore}_H = \text{LordScore} + 15.0$.
2. **Loop Returning to Self (*Parivartana*):** Restricted strictly to true 2-planet mutual sign exchanges ($\text{hops\_to\_repeat} == 2$):
   * If *Mahā Parivartana* (no Dusthāna involved): $(0.80 \times \text{LordScore} + 0.20 \times \text{PartnerScore}) + 15.0$.
   * If *Dainya Parivartana* (Dusthāna exchange with good house): $(0.80 \times \text{LordScore} + 0.20 \times \text{PartnerScore}) - 10.0$.
3. **Fallback to Final Dispositor (including loops with $\text{hops} \ge 3$):**
   $$\text{EffectiveLordScore}_H = 0.65 \times \text{LordScore} + 0.20 \times \text{ImmediateDispScore} + 0.15 \times \text{FinalDispScore}$$
4. **House Lord Digbala Bonus:** Adds $+0.15 \times \text{DigBalaPts}_{\text{Lord}}$.
5. **Direct Lord-in-Dusthāna Structural Penalty (*Phaladīpikā* 15.18 & 6.57):**
   * Applied when the lord is in a Dusthāna from its own house ($r_{\text{lord}} \in [6, 8, 12]$) ($-12.0$ for R8, $-8.0$ for R6/R12) or in an unmitigated 12th house placement ($-8.0$).
   * Doubled only if $d_{\text{D1}} < 0$ and $\text{LordScore} \ge 0$ (avoiding double-counting when `LordScore` is already negative).
   * Waived if the lord is in own/exaltation sign or forms a dignified Viparīta Rāja Yoga ($d_{\text{D1}} \ge 0.40$).

#### 3. Sign-Correct Occupant Synthesis
Each non-lord occupant is evaluated by its effective house polarity ($+1.0$ for 1st lord in non-Dusthānas, $+0.50$ for malefics in Upacayas, $-1.0$ for malefics in non-Upacayas):
* **Deadband for Moon / Mild Benefics:** When $F \in [-0.15, +0.15]$, polarity is positive ($+1.0$) if $d_{\text{D1}} \ge 0$, and malefic ($-1.0$) only if $d_{\text{D1}} < 0$.
* For positive polarity ($\text{polarity} > 0$): $\text{occ\_contrib} = \text{Score}_P \times \text{polarity}$.
* For negative polarity ($\text{polarity} < 0$, non-lord malefic in non-Upacaya house per *Phaladīpikā* 15.1 & 15.18):
  * If $\text{Score}_P \ge 0$ (dignified malefic): $\text{occ\_contrib} = -45.0 \times \left(1.0 - 0.50 \times \min\left(1.0, \frac{\text{Score}_P}{100.0}\right)\right)$ (softened bad results up to half).
  * If $\text{Score}_P < 0$ (afflicted malefic): $\text{occ\_contrib} = -45.0 \times \left(1.0 + \min\left(1.0, \frac{|\text{Score}_P|}{100.0}\right)\right)$ (intensified bad results up to $2\times$).
$$\text{CombinedLordScore}_H = \begin{cases} 0.70 \times \text{EffectiveLordScore}_H + 0.30 \times \overline{\text{OccupantImpacts}} & \text{if non-lord occupants exist} \\ \text{EffectiveLordScore}_H & \text{otherwise} \end{cases}$$

---

## 3. Qualitative Health Tiers (`synthesis.health_rating`)

* **$\ge +100.0$:** `"Exceptional / Off the Chart"` (`classical_tier`: `"Puṣṭa"`)
* **$+25.0 \text{ to } +99.9$:** `"Flourishing / Fortified"` (`classical_tier`: `"Puṣṭa"`)
* **$-20.0 \text{ to } +24.9$:** `"Mixed / Resilient"` (`classical_tier`: `"Miśra"`)
* **$-99.9 \text{ to } -20.1$:** `"Strained / Challenged"` (`classical_tier`: `"Hīna"`)
* **$\le -100.0$:** `"Severely Afflicted / Obstructed"` (`classical_tier`: `"Hīna"`)

"""
jyotish/report/houses_of_life.py

Stage 4 Report Engine: Vic DiCara's "Houses of Life" Quantitative House & Planetary
Evaluation System (based on Phaladīpikā Chapters 3, 4, 6, and 15).

Consumes the read-only serialized chart_dict from ChartPipeline.to_dict() without
modifying any Frozen Core Calculus modules (Stages 1-3).
"""

import math
import unicodedata
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Tuple, Optional
import jyotish.aspects.aspects as aspects
from jyotish.baseline import (
    ZODIAC_SIGNS,
    SIGN_LORDS,
    COMBUSTION_ORBS,
    COMBUSTION_ORBS_RETROGRADE,
    EXALTATION_SIGNS,
)
from jyotish.relationships.relationships import get_natural_relationship

CLASSICAL_PLANETS: List[str] = [
    "Sun", "Moon", "Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Venus", "Saturn"
]

# Moolatrikona root sign per Phaladeepika Ch. 15 / DiCara rules
DICARA_MOOLATRIKONA_SIGNS: Dict[str, str] = {
    "Sun": "Leo",
    "Moon": "Taurus",       # Exaltation sign serves as root for Moon
    "Mars": "Aries",
    "Mercury": "Virgo",     # Female sign exception for Mercury
    "Jupiter": "Sagittarius",
    "Venus": "Libra",
    "Saturn": "Aquarius",
}

# Regular domicile signs for dual-ruling planets
DICARA_DOMICILE_SIGNS: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {
    "Sun": None,
    "Moon": "Cancer",
    "Mars": "Scorpio",
    "Mercury": "Gemini",
    "Jupiter": "Pisces",
    "Venus": "Taurus",
    "Saturn": "Capricorn",
}

# D1 Rashi Dignity mapping to DiCara's [-1.0, +1.0] scale
DICARA_D1_DIGNITY_MAP: Dict[str, float] = {
    "Exalted": 1.0,
    "Deep Exaltation": 1.0,
    "Moolatrikona": 0.9,
    "Own Sign": 0.8,
    "Great Friend": 0.6,
    "Great Friend's Sign": 0.6,
    "Friend": 0.4,
    "Friend's Sign": 0.4,
    "Neutral": 0.0,
    "Neutral's Sign": 0.0,
    "Enemy": -0.4,
    "Enemy's Sign": -0.4,
    "Great Enemy": -0.7,
    "Great Enemy's Sign": -0.7,
    "Debilitated": -1.0,
    "Deep Debilitation": -1.0,
}

# Base natural temperaments for Rule 4
NATURAL_TEMPERAMENT: Dict[str, float] = {
    "Jupiter": 1.0,
    "Venus": 1.0,
    "Mars": -1.0,
    "Saturn": -1.0,
    "Sun": -0.5,
    "Moon": 0.5,
    "Mercury": 0.5,
}

# Digbala canonical peak houses per Phaladeepika Ch. 4
DIGBALA_PEAK_HOUSES: Dict[str, int] = {
    "Jupiter": 1,
    "Mercury": 1,
    "Sun": 10,
    "Mars": 10,
    "Saturn": 7,
    "Moon": 4,
    "Venus": 4,
}

# Bhava Karakas per Phaladeepika Ch. 15 / DiCara disclosures
DICARA_HOUSE_KARAKAS: Dict[int, List[str]] = {
    1: ["Sun"],
    2: ["Jupiter"],
    3: ["Mars"],
    4: ["Moon", "Mercury"],       # Phaladeepika 15.15-16 merges Moon & Mercury for 4th
    5: ["Jupiter"],
    6: ["Saturn", "Mars"],
    7: ["Venus"],
    8: ["Saturn"],
    9: ["Jupiter", "Sun"],
    10: ["Mercury", "Sun", "Jupiter", "Saturn"],
    11: ["Jupiter"],
    12: ["Saturn"],
}


def _shortest_distance(lon1: float, lon2: float) -> float:
    """Calculates the minimal angular distance between two longitudes on a 360-degree circle."""
    diff = abs(lon1 - lon2) % 360.0
    return 360.0 - diff if diff > 180.0 else diff


def _shortest_house_dist(h1: int, h2: int) -> int:
    """Calculates the minimal house distance between two houses on a 12-house circle."""
    diff = abs(h1 - h2) % 12
    return 12 - diff if diff > 6 else diff


def _get_dignity_status(p_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Returns compound Pancadha Maitri final_dignity if available, else base dignity."""
    bd = p_data.get("dignity_breakdown")
    if isinstance(bd, dict) and bd.get("final_dignity"):
        return str(bd["final_dignity"])
    return str(p_data.get("dignity") or "Neutral")


def _get_composite_dignity_unit(
    p_name: str,
    chart_dict: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    d1_grahas: Dict[str, Any]
) -> float:
    """
    Computes multi-varga dignity blending D1 and D9 (Navāṁśa per Phaladīpikā 3.4 & 3.11).
    Accounts for Vargottama (acting like own sign per Phaladīpikā 3.4, floored at 0.80).
    """
    d1_status = _get_dignity_status(d1_grahas.get(p_name, {}))
    d_d1 = DICARA_D1_DIGNITY_MAP.get(d1_status, 0.0)

    if not chart_dict:
        return d_d1

    d9_grahas = chart_dict.get("vargas", {}).get("D9", {}).get("grahas", {})
    if p_name in d9_grahas:
        d9_status = _get_dignity_status(d9_grahas[p_name])
        d_d9 = DICARA_D1_DIGNITY_MAP.get(d9_status, 0.0)
        d1_sign = d1_grahas.get(p_name, {}).get("sign")
        d9_sign = d9_grahas[p_name].get("sign")
        is_vargottama = (d1_sign is not None and d1_sign == d9_sign)

        if is_vargottama:
            effective_d = max(d_d1, 0.80)
        else:
            effective_d = 0.65 * d_d1 + 0.35 * d_d9
        return max(-1.0, min(1.0, effective_d))

    return d_d1


def _get_bala_and_digbala_bonus(
    p_name: str,
    p_house: int,
    chart_dict: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
) -> Tuple[float, float]:
    """
    Extracts canonical Digbala factor in [-1.0, +1.0] and returns (bala_ratio, digbala_pts).
    Formula: DigFactor_P = 1.0 - (shortest_house_dist(H_P, H_peak) / 3.0)
    digbala_pts = 20.0 * DigFactor_P
    """
    h_peak = DIGBALA_PEAK_HOUSES.get(p_name, 1)
    dist = _shortest_house_dist(p_house, h_peak)
    dig_factor = 1.0 - (dist / 3.0)
    digbala_pts = 20.0 * dig_factor

    bala_ratio = 1.0
    if chart_dict and "shadbala" in chart_dict:
        sb_entry = chart_dict["shadbala"].get(p_name, {})
        if isinstance(sb_entry, dict) and "shadbala_ratio" in sb_entry:
            bala_ratio = float(sb_entry["shadbala_ratio"])
            clamped_bala = max(0.75, min(1.25, bala_ratio))
            if digbala_pts >= 0.0:
                digbala_pts = digbala_pts * clamped_bala
            else:
                digbala_pts = digbala_pts / clamped_bala
            digbala_pts += (clamped_bala - 1.0) * 20.0

    return bala_ratio, digbala_pts


def _extract_whole_sign_geometry(chart_dict: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Extracts Whole Sign house geometry (Phase 1).
    Ascendant degree inside its sign defines the sensitive cusp for all 12 houses.
    """
    d1 = chart_dict["vargas"]["D1"]
    lagna = d1["lagna"]
    asc_sign = lagna["sign"]
    asc_deg_in_sign = float(lagna.get("degree_0_to_30", lagna["longitude"] % 30.0))
    asc_sign_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(asc_sign)

    d1_grahas = d1["grahas"]

    houses: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for h in range(1, 13):
        sign_idx = (asc_sign_idx + h - 1) % 12
        sign_name = ZODIAC_SIGNS[sign_idx]
        lord = SIGN_LORDS[sign_name]
        cusp_lon = (sign_idx * 30.0 + asc_deg_in_sign) % 360.0

        occupants = [
            p for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
            if d1_grahas.get(p, {}).get("sign") == sign_name
        ]

        houses[h] = {
            "house": h,
            "sign": sign_name,
            "sign_index": sign_idx,
            "lord": lord,
            "cusp_longitude": round(cusp_lon, 4),
            "cusp_degree": round(asc_deg_in_sign, 4),
            "occupants": occupants
        }

    return {
        "ascendant_sign": asc_sign,
        "ascendant_degree": round(asc_deg_in_sign, 4),
        "ascendant_longitude": round(float(lagna["longitude"]), 4),
        "houses": houses
    }


def calculate_functional_nature(
    reference_sign: str,
    d1_grahas: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Implements Phase 2: Planetary Functional Nature Quantification (F_P).
    Incorporates Phaladīpikā Ch. 15 Rules 1-4, Svakṣetra dual-lordship overrides (15.29),
    Moon Pakṣa Bala elongation, Mercury malefic association, and BPHS Ch. 34 Yogakaraka adjustments.
    """
    ref_idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(reference_sign)

    def house_of_sign(sign_name: str) -> int:
        idx = ZODIAC_SIGNS.index(sign_name)
        return ((idx - ref_idx) % 12) + 1

    results: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    # Calculate Moon brightness (Pakṣa Bala) if chart planetary positions exist
    moon_inherent = 0.5
    if d1_grahas and "Moon" in d1_grahas and "Sun" in d1_grahas:
        try:
            m_lon = float(d1_grahas["Moon"]["longitude"])
            s_lon = float(d1_grahas["Sun"]["longitude"])
            delta_moon_sun = _shortest_distance(m_lon, s_lon)
            paksa_factor = delta_moon_sun / 180.0
            if delta_moon_sun >= 165.0:
                moon_inherent = 1.0
            else:
                moon_inherent = round(-0.5 + 1.5 * paksa_factor, 4)
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            moon_inherent = 0.5

    # Check Mercury condition: afflicted if within 10 deg of malefic in same sign
    merc_inherent = 0.5
    if d1_grahas and "Mercury" in d1_grahas:
        try:
            merc_sign = d1_grahas["Mercury"].get("sign")
            merc_lon = float(d1_grahas["Mercury"]["longitude"])
            for mal in ["Mars", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu"]:
                if mal in d1_grahas and d1_grahas[mal].get("sign") == merc_sign:
                    mal_lon = float(d1_grahas[mal]["longitude"])
                    if _shortest_distance(merc_lon, mal_lon) <= 10.0:
                        merc_inherent = -0.5
                        break
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            merc_inherent = 0.5

    for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
        mt_sign = DICARA_MOOLATRIKONA_SIGNS[p]
        dom_sign = DICARA_DOMICILE_SIGNS[p]

        # Owned houses for actual sign lordships (Moon owns Cancer; Taurus is MT root)
        owned_houses: List[int] = []
        if p == "Sun":
            owned_houses = [house_of_sign("Leo")]
        elif p == "Moon":
            owned_houses = [house_of_sign("Cancer")]
        else:
            owned_houses = [house_of_sign(mt_sign)]
            if dom_sign:
                owned_houses.append(house_of_sign(dom_sign))

        # Base inherent temperament
        if p == "Moon":
            inherent_score = moon_inherent
        elif p == "Mercury":
            inherent_score = merc_inherent
        elif p == "Sun":
            inherent_score = -0.5
        else:
            inherent_score = NATURAL_TEMPERAMENT.get(p, 0.0)

        # Rule 1: 1st House Lord check (unconditional flat +3)
        if 1 in owned_houses:
            results[p] = {
                "score": 3.0,
                "classification": "Benefic",
                "rule_breakdown": {
                    "is_first_lord": True,
                    "trine_score": 0.0,
                    "dusthana_score": 0.0,
                    "inherent_score": inherent_score
                }
            }
            continue

        # Rule 2 & 3: Trines (5, 9) and Dusthānas (6, 8, 12)
        trine_score = 0.0
        dusthana_score = 0.0

        h_mt = house_of_sign(mt_sign)
        h_dom = house_of_sign(dom_sign) if dom_sign else None

        # Special Edge Case for Moon: Sagittarius Lagna (Taurus = H6, Cancer = H8)
        if p == "Moon" and h_mt in [6, 8, 12] and h_dom in [6, 8, 12]:
            dusthana_score = -2.0
        else:
            if h_mt in [5, 9]:
                trine_score += 2.0
            elif h_mt in [6, 8, 12]:
                dusthana_score -= 2.0

            if h_dom:
                if h_dom in [5, 9]:
                    trine_score += 1.0
                elif h_dom in [6, 8, 12]:
                    dusthana_score -= 1.0

        # Svakṣetra Dual-Lordship Override (Phaladīpikā 15.29):
        # When a planet physically occupies one of its own signs corresponding to a non-Dusthāna house,
        # its secondary Dusthāna lordship blemish is waived.
        if d1_grahas and p in d1_grahas:
            p_sign = d1_grahas[p].get("sign")
            valid_own = [mt_sign]
            if dom_sign:
                valid_own.append(dom_sign)
            if p_sign in valid_own:
                h_occ = house_of_sign(p_sign)
                if h_occ not in [6, 8, 12]:
                    dusthana_score = 0.0

        # Rule 4: Inherent temperament + Classical Yogakāraka adjustment (BPHS Ch. 34)
        owns_kendra = any(h in [4, 7, 10] for h in owned_houses)
        owns_trine = any(h in [5, 9] for h in owned_houses)

        if owns_kendra and owns_trine:
            # Supreme Raja Yogakaraka (Mars for Cancer/Leo, Saturn for Taurus/Libra)
            inherent_score = max(0.0, inherent_score) + 1.0

        net_f = trine_score + dusthana_score + inherent_score
        classification = "Benefic" if net_f > 0 else ("Malefic" if net_f < 0 else "Neutral")

        results[p] = {
            "score": round(net_f, 2),
            "classification": classification,
            "rule_breakdown": {
                "is_first_lord": False,
                "trine_score": round(trine_score, 2),
                "dusthana_score": round(dusthana_score, 2),
                "inherent_score": round(inherent_score, 2)
            }
        }

    return results


def scale_by_dignity(base_val: float, dignity_unit: float, functional_score: float) -> float:
    """
    Universal Dignity-Scaling Law (Phase 3).
    Scales a non-negative base magnitude by the planet's dignity d_P in [-1.0, +1.0]
    according to functional score F.
    Severe debilitation (d = -1.0) retains only 0.20x of good results per Phaladīpikā 15.18.
    """
    b = abs(base_val)
    d = max(-1.0, min(1.0, float(dignity_unit)))

    if functional_score == 0.0:
        return b * d
    elif functional_score > 0:
        if d >= 0:
            return b * (1.0 + d)
        else:
            return b * (1.0 - 0.80 * abs(d))
    else:  # functional_score < 0
        if d <= 0:
            return b * (1.0 + abs(d))
        else:
            return b * (1.0 - d / 2.0)


def _compute_signed_ray_impact(
    base_val: float,
    dignity_unit: float,
    functional_score: float,
    slope: float = 0.65
) -> float:
    """
    Computes smooth continuous signed ray impact across the functional spectrum,
    eliminating cliff edges and deadband sign inversions across [-0.25, +0.25].
    """
    b = abs(base_val)
    d = max(-1.0, min(1.0, float(dignity_unit)))
    f = float(functional_score)

    if f >= 0.25:
        scaled_pos = scale_by_dignity(b, d, 1.0)
        f_mult = 1.0 + slope * (f - 1.0)
        return scaled_pos * f_mult
    elif f <= -0.25:
        scaled_neg = scale_by_dignity(b, d, -1.0)
        f_mult = -(1.0 + slope * (abs(f) - 1.0))
        return scaled_neg * f_mult
    else:
        signed_zero = b * d
        if f >= 0.0:
            w = f / 0.25
            val_pos = scale_by_dignity(b, d, 1.0) * (1.0 + slope * (0.25 - 1.0))
            return (1.0 - w) * signed_zero + w * val_pos
        else:
            w = -f / 0.25
            val_neg = scale_by_dignity(b, d, -1.0) * -(1.0 + slope * (0.25 - 1.0))
            return (1.0 - w) * signed_zero + w * val_neg


def calculate_cusp_and_planet_influences(
    geometry: Dict[str, Any],
    d1_grahas: Dict[str, Any],
    adv_aspects: Dict[str, Any]
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Computes degree-specific aspects and conjunctions on a 0-100% scale (Phase 4),
    applying cross-border halving and multi-planet cusp dominance (potency stealing).
    """
    planet_to_planet: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {p: {} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}

    # 1. Planet to Planet interactions
    for sender in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
        s_lon = float(d1_grahas[sender]["longitude"])
        s_sign = d1_grahas[sender]["sign"]

        for receiver in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            if sender == receiver:
                continue
            r_lon = float(d1_grahas[receiver]["longitude"])
            r_sign = d1_grahas[receiver]["sign"]

            # Aspect Virūpas
            virupas = 0.0
            p_aspects = adv_aspects.get("planets", {}).get(sender, {})
            if receiver in p_aspects:
                entry = p_aspects[receiver]
                virupas = entry.get("raw", 0.0) if isinstance(entry, dict) else float(entry)
            else:
                virupas = aspects.get_graha_drishti(sender, s_lon, r_lon)

            aspect_pct = (virupas / 60.0) * 100.0

            # Conjunction within 30 deg
            dist = _shortest_distance(s_lon, r_lon)
            conj_pct = 0.0
            if dist < 30.0:
                raw_c = (1.0 - dist / 30.0) * 100.0
                conj_pct = raw_c * 0.5 if s_sign != r_sign else raw_c

            # Mutual sign exchange (Parivartana / Mitho Yoga per Phaladīpikā 15.30)
            is_parivartana = (
                s_sign != r_sign
                and SIGN_LORDS.get(s_sign) == receiver
                and SIGN_LORDS.get(r_sign) == sender
            )
            pariv_pct = 50.0 if is_parivartana else 0.0

            planet_to_planet[sender][receiver] = max(aspect_pct, conj_pct, pariv_pct)

    # 2. Planet to House Cusp interactions with Multi-Planet Cusp Dominance
    raw_conjunctions: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {p: {} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
    adjusted_conjunctions: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {p: {} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
    planet_to_cusp: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {p: {} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
    aspect_to_cusp: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {p: {} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
    aspect_virupas_to_cusp: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]] = {p: {} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}

    houses_dict = geometry["houses"]

    # Compute raw conjunctions and aspect percentages to each house cusp
    for h, h_data in houses_dict.items():
        cusp_lon = h_data["cusp_longitude"]
        occupants = h_data["occupants"]

        # Raw conjunction for occupants in this sign (linear 30° orb per Phase 4.2)
        occ_raw: Dict[str, float] = {}
        for p in occupants:
            p_lon = float(d1_grahas[p]["longitude"])
            dist = _shortest_distance(p_lon, cusp_lon)
            raw_c = max(0.0, (1.0 - dist / 30.0) * 100.0)
            raw_conjunctions[p][h] = raw_c
            occ_raw[p] = raw_c

        # Multi-planet cusp dominance stealing within the sign (order-independent pairwise stealing)
        if len(occupants) == 1:
            p_solo = occupants[0]
            adjusted_conjunctions[p_solo][h] = occ_raw[p_solo]
        elif len(occupants) >= 2:
            sorted_occ = sorted(occupants, key=lambda p: occ_raw[p], reverse=True)
            cur_conjunctions = {p: occ_raw[p] for p in sorted_occ}

            for i in range(len(sorted_occ)):
                near_p = sorted_occ[i]
                near_lon = float(d1_grahas[near_p]["longitude"])
                for j in range(i + 1, len(sorted_occ)):
                    far_p = sorted_occ[j]
                    far_lon = float(d1_grahas[far_p]["longitude"])
                    dist_between = _shortest_distance(near_lon, far_lon)
                    c_near = cur_conjunctions[near_p]
                    c_far = cur_conjunctions[far_p]

                    if dist_between < 15.0 and occ_raw[near_p] > occ_raw[far_p] and (c_near + c_far) > 0:
                        orb_factor = 1.0 - (dist_between / 15.0)
                        steal = c_far * (c_near / (c_near + c_far)) * orb_factor
                        steal = min(steal, cur_conjunctions[far_p])
                        avail_cap = max(0.0, 125.0 - cur_conjunctions[near_p])
                        steal = min(steal, avail_cap)
                        cur_conjunctions[far_p] = max(0.0, cur_conjunctions[far_p] - steal)
                        cur_conjunctions[near_p] = min(125.0, cur_conjunctions[near_p] + steal)

            for p in occupants:
                adjusted_conjunctions[p][h] = cur_conjunctions[p]

        # Non-occupants: cross-border conjunction (if within 30 deg) and Graha Drishti aspect
        for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            if p in occupants:
                continue
            p_lon = float(d1_grahas[p]["longitude"])
            dist = _shortest_distance(p_lon, cusp_lon)
            raw_c = max(0.0, (1.0 - dist / 30.0) * 100.0) if dist < 30.0 else 0.0
            # Spans across sign boundary -> halved
            halved_c = raw_c * 0.5
            raw_conjunctions[p][h] = halved_c
            adjusted_conjunctions[p][h] = halved_c

        # Final planet_to_cusp impact: pure Parāśara Sphuṭa Dṛṣṭi degree-based aspect
        for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            p_lon = float(d1_grahas[p]["longitude"])
            virupas = aspects.get_graha_drishti(p, p_lon, cusp_lon)
            asp_pct = (virupas / 60.0) * 100.0
            aspect_to_cusp[p][h] = round(asp_pct, 2)
            aspect_virupas_to_cusp[p][h] = round(virupas, 2)
            final_c = adjusted_conjunctions[p][h]
            planet_to_cusp[p][h] = max(asp_pct, final_c)

    return {
        "planet_to_planet": planet_to_planet,
        "planet_to_cusp": planet_to_cusp,
        "aspect_to_cusp": aspect_to_cusp,
        "aspect_virupas_to_cusp": aspect_virupas_to_cusp,
        "raw_conjunctions": raw_conjunctions,
        "adjusted_conjunctions": adjusted_conjunctions
    }


def evaluate_planets_hol(
    geometry: Dict[str, Any],
    d1_grahas: Dict[str, Any],
    func_natures: Dict[str, Any],
    influences: Dict[str, Any],
    chart_dict: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Implements Phase 5: Comprehensive Planetary Assessment across 5 factors.
    Strictly separates Intrinsic Quality (Dignity, Aspects, Digbala) from
    Prominence (Cusp Alignment & House Category), preventing Kendra cusps from
    flipping debilitated planets positive. Incorporates Phaladīpikā 3.9 vilagna protection.
    """
    planet_results: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    p2p = influences["planet_to_planet"]
    adj_conj = influences["adjusted_conjunctions"]
    raw_conj = influences["raw_conjunctions"]

    # First pass: gather combustion losses
    sun_lon = float(d1_grahas["Sun"]["longitude"])
    combustion_losses: Dict[str, float] = {}

    for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
        if p == "Sun":
            continue
        p_data = d1_grahas[p]
        p_lon = float(p_data["longitude"])

        dist_sun = p_data.get("sun_distance")
        if dist_sun is None:
            dist_sun = _shortest_distance(p_lon, sun_lon)
        else:
            dist_sun = float(dist_sun)

        orb = p_data.get("combustion_orb")
        if orb is None:
            is_retro = p_data.get("is_retrograde", False)
            if p == "Venus" and is_retro:
                orb = 8.0
            elif p == "Mercury" and is_retro:
                orb = 12.0
            else:
                orb = COMBUSTION_ORBS.get(p, 10.0)
        else:
            orb = float(orb)

        if dist_sun <= orb and orb > 0:
            loss = max(0.0, min(1.0, 1.0 - (dist_sun / orb)))
        else:
            loss = 0.0
        combustion_losses[p] = loss

    solar_gain_sum = sum(combustion_losses.values())

    # Second pass: compute full planetary condition
    for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
        p_data = d1_grahas[p]
        p_lon = float(p_data["longitude"])
        p_sign = p_data["sign"]
        p_deg_in_sign = float(p_data.get("degree_0_to_30", p_data["longitude"] % 30.0))
        p_ruled = p_data.get("ruled_houses", [])

        # Find house occupied
        p_house = 1
        for h, h_data in geometry["houses"].items():
            if h_data["sign"] == p_sign:
                p_house = h
                break

        # 1. Multi-Varga Composite Dignity (D1 + D9 per Phaladīpikā 3.4 & 3.11)
        d_p = _get_composite_dignity_unit(p, chart_dict, d1_grahas)
        d_pct = d_p * 100.0
        dignity_status = _get_dignity_status(p_data)
        d_d1 = DICARA_D1_DIGNITY_MAP.get(dignity_status, 0.0)

        # 3. Canonical Digbala & Bala Points (Phaladīpikā Ch. 4)
        _, digbala_pts = _get_bala_and_digbala_bonus(p, p_house, chart_dict)

        # 2. Combined Incoming Influences
        incoming_breakdown: Dict[str, float] = {}
        raw_net_inf = 0.0
        for q in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            if q == p:
                continue
            i_val = p2p[q][p]
            d_q = _get_composite_dignity_unit(q, chart_dict, d1_grahas)
            f_q = func_natures[q]["score"]

            # Smooth continuous signed ray impact across functional spectrum (slope=0.20 for planet-to-planet)
            signed_impact = _compute_signed_ray_impact(i_val, d_q, f_q, slope=0.20)

            # Attenuate same-house Dusthāna conjunction boosts by 0.50x if planet is in Enemy/Debility with negative Digbala
            q_sign = d1_grahas[q]["sign"]
            q_house = 1
            for h_chk, h_data_chk in geometry["houses"].items():
                if h_data_chk["sign"] == q_sign:
                    q_house = h_chk
                    break

            if d_d1 < 0 and p_house in [6, 8, 12] and digbala_pts < 0:
                if q_house == p_house and signed_impact > 0:
                    signed_impact *= 0.50

            incoming_breakdown[q] = round(signed_impact, 2)
            raw_net_inf += signed_impact

        # Nodal Conjunction & Eclipse Affliction (Phaladīpikā Ch. 3 / Ch. 15)
        eclipse_loss = 0.0
        p_lon = float(p_data["longitude"])
        for node in ["Rahu", "Ketu"]:
            if node in d1_grahas and d1_grahas[node].get("sign") == p_sign:
                n_lon = float(d1_grahas[node].get("longitude", 0.0))
                dist_to_node = _shortest_distance(p_lon, n_lon)
                if dist_to_node <= 15.0:
                    node_conj = (1.0 - dist_to_node / 15.0) * 100.0
                    if p in ["Sun", "Moon"]:
                        node_pen = 0.85 * node_conj
                        loss_factor = 0.15 * (1.0 - dist_to_node / 15.0)
                        if p == "Sun" and p_house in [3, 6, 11] and d_p >= 0.4:
                            node_pen *= 0.50
                            loss_factor *= 0.50
                        if dist_to_node <= 12.0:
                            eclipse_loss = max(eclipse_loss, loss_factor)
                    else:
                        node_pen = 0.50 * node_conj
                        if p_house in [3, 6, 11]:
                            node_pen *= 0.50
                    incoming_breakdown[node] = round(-node_pen, 2)
                    raw_net_inf -= node_pen

        net_inf = max(-150.0, min(150.0, raw_net_inf / 2.5))

        # 4. Intrinsic Quality Base (Quality does NOT include raw cusp proximity points)
        quality_base = 0.50 * d_pct + 0.35 * net_inf + 0.15 * digbala_pts

        # 5. Cusp Alignment & House Placement Prominence
        raw_c = raw_conj[p].get(p_house, 0.0)
        adj_c = adj_conj[p].get(p_house, 0.0)
        capped_base = min(100.0, adj_c)
        cusp_prox_factor = 0.80 + 0.40 * (capped_base / 100.0)

        if p_house in [1, 4, 5, 9, 10]:
            h_category = "Prominent"
            base_h_mult = 1.25
        elif p_house in [2, 7, 11]:
            h_category = "Regular"
            base_h_mult = 1.00
        else:  # [3, 6, 8, 12]
            h_category = "Hidden"
            if p_house in [6, 8, 12] and any(rh in [6, 8, 12] for rh in p_ruled):
                base_h_mult = 1.00  # Dusthāna lord in Dusthāna (Viparīta resilience)
            elif p_house == 3 and (3 in p_ruled or p in ["Sun", "Mars", "Saturn"]):
                base_h_mult = 0.90  # 3rd lord or natural malefics in 3rd Upacaya
            elif p_house == 3:
                base_h_mult = 0.75  # Mild Upacaya house
            elif p_house == 6 and p in ["Sun", "Mars", "Saturn"]:
                base_h_mult = 0.85  # Natural malefics in 6th Upacaya conquer obstacles
            elif p_house == 12 and (p == "Venus" or d_p >= 0.4):
                base_h_mult = 0.90  # Venus or dignified planet in 12th
            else:
                base_h_mult = 0.75  # Moderated Dusthāna expression
            if p_house in [6, 8, 12] and d_d1 < 0:
                base_h_mult *= 0.70

        effective_h_mult = base_h_mult * cusp_prox_factor

        # Apply House Placement Modifier to QualityBase
        if quality_base >= 0.0:
            base_avg = quality_base * effective_h_mult
        else:
            if p_house == 1:
                # Phaladīpikā 3.9 vilagna protection: softens negative dignity strain in 1st house by 0.5x
                base_avg = quality_base * 0.50 * cusp_prox_factor
            elif p_house in [4, 5, 9, 10]:
                base_avg = quality_base * effective_h_mult
            elif p_house in [6, 8, 12]:
                if any(rh in [6, 8, 12] for rh in p_ruled):
                    base_avg = quality_base * 0.90 * cusp_prox_factor
                else:
                    base_avg = quality_base * 1.20 * cusp_prox_factor
            else:
                base_avg = quality_base * effective_h_mult

        # 6. Bhāva Sandhi (4.0 deg border threshold, relieved by Cusp Proximity per Phaladīpikā 15.14 & 8.34-35)
        dist_border = min(p_deg_in_sign, 30.0 - p_deg_in_sign)
        if dist_border < 4.0:
            raw_sandhi_pen = max(0.0, 1.0 - (dist_border / 4.0))
            cusp_lon_h = float(geometry["houses"][p_house].get("cusp_longitude", p_lon))
            cusp_deg = cusp_lon_h % 30.0
            cusp_dist_border = min(cusp_deg, 30.0 - cusp_deg)
            dist_to_cusp = _shortest_distance(p_lon, cusp_lon_h)

            # Phaladīpikā 15.14 & 8.34-35:
            # If the house cusp itself is near the border (< 4.0°) and the planet is inside the house
            # between the cusp and sign center (dist_border >= cusp_dist_border), or tightly conjunct
            # the cusp (dist_to_cusp <= 5.0), the planet is at the cusp door, not drifting into the junction.
            if (cusp_dist_border < 4.0 and dist_border >= cusp_dist_border) or dist_to_cusp <= 5.0:
                cusp_relief = 0.0
            elif dist_to_cusp < 10.0:
                cusp_relief = (dist_to_cusp - 5.0) / 5.0
            else:
                cusp_relief = 1.0

            sandhi_penalty = raw_sandhi_pen * cusp_relief
            in_sandhi = sandhi_penalty > 0.0
        else:
            sandhi_penalty = 0.0
            in_sandhi = False
        retention_multiplier = 1.0 - sandhi_penalty

        # 7. Combustion & Solar Transfer
        if p == "Sun":
            is_combust = False
            dist_sun = 0.0
            tolerance_orb = None
            loss_ratio = 0.0
            solar_gain_ratio = round(min(0.50, solar_gain_sum * 0.25), 4)
            combustion_multiplier = 1.0 + solar_gain_ratio
        else:
            loss_ratio = combustion_losses.get(p, 0.0)
            is_combust = loss_ratio > 0.0
            dist_sun = float(p_data.get("sun_distance", _shortest_distance(float(p_data["longitude"]), sun_lon)))
            tolerance_orb = p_data.get("combustion_orb")
            solar_gain_ratio = 0.0
            combustion_multiplier = 1.0 - loss_ratio

        # Synthesis (Sign-Safe Application of Sandhi, Combustion & Eclipse)
        if base_avg >= 0.0:
            if p == "Sun":
                final_assessment = base_avg * (1.0 - 0.5 * sandhi_penalty) * combustion_multiplier * (1.0 - eclipse_loss)
            else:
                final_assessment = base_avg * (1.0 - 0.5 * sandhi_penalty) * (1.0 - 0.75 * loss_ratio) * (1.0 - eclipse_loss)
        else:
            if p == "Sun":
                strain_mult = 1.0 + 0.25 * sandhi_penalty + eclipse_loss
                final_assessment = (base_avg * strain_mult) + (10.0 * solar_gain_ratio)
            else:
                strain_mult = 1.0 + 0.25 * sandhi_penalty + 0.35 * loss_ratio + eclipse_loss
                final_assessment = base_avg * strain_mult

        planet_results[p] = {
            "sign": p_sign,
            "house": p_house,
            "degree_in_sign": round(p_deg_in_sign, 2),
            "ruled_houses": p_ruled,
            "functional_nature": func_natures[p],
            "dignity": {
                "status": dignity_status,
                "unit_score": round(d_p, 2),
                "pct_score": round(d_pct, 2)
            },
            "combined_influences": {
                "net_score": round(net_inf, 2),
                "incoming_breakdown": incoming_breakdown
            },
            "cusp_alignment": {
                "raw_conjunction_pct": round(raw_c, 2),
                "dominance_adjusted_pct": round(adj_c, 2),
                "capped_base_pct": round(capped_base, 2),
                "house_category": h_category,
                "house_multiplier": round(effective_h_mult, 4),
                "alignment_score": round(capped_base * effective_h_mult, 2),
                "digbala_points": round(digbala_pts, 2),
            },
            "bhava_sandhi": {
                "distance_to_border": round(dist_border, 2),
                "in_sandhi_zone": in_sandhi,
                "penalty_ratio": round(sandhi_penalty, 4),
                "retention_multiplier": round(retention_multiplier, 4)
            },
            "combustion": {
                "is_combust": is_combust,
                "distance_to_sun": round(dist_sun, 2),
                "tolerance_orb": tolerance_orb,
                "loss_ratio": round(loss_ratio, 4),
                "solar_gain_ratio": solar_gain_ratio,
                "combustion_multiplier": round(combustion_multiplier, 4)
            },
            "eclipse": {
                "is_eclipsed": eclipse_loss > 0.0,
                "loss_ratio": round(eclipse_loss, 4)
            },
            "quality_base_score": round(quality_base, 2),
            "cusp_proximity_factor": round(cusp_prox_factor, 2),
            "base_average_score": round(base_avg, 2),
            "final_assessment_score": round(final_assessment, 2)
        }

    return planet_results


def evaluate_basic_house_conditions(
    geometry: Dict[str, Any],
    func_natures: Dict[str, Any],
    d1_grahas: Dict[str, Any],
    influences: Dict[str, Any],
    planets_eval: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
) -> Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Implements Phase 6: Basic House Condition — Direct Evaluation.
    Condition-gates cusp influences by combustion and Bhāva Sandhi (Phaladīpikā 15.1 amūḍha-hīnāḥ).
    Transforms natural malefics in Upacayas (3, 6, 11) into resilience boosters (15.18).
    Evaluates Rāhu & Ketu cusp occupancy, proximity, and malefic aggravation (15.8).
    Uses a fixed baseline divisor (4.0) with hyperbolic tangent saturation guaranteeing strict monotonicity.
    Generates a spreadsheet-grade calculation_ledger documenting every row, column, and footer proof.
    """
    house_results: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    p2c = influences["planet_to_cusp"]
    asp_to_cusp_map = influences.get("aspect_to_cusp", {})
    asp_virupas_map = influences.get("aspect_virupas_to_cusp", {})
    raw_conj_map = influences.get("raw_conjunctions", {})
    adj_conj_map = influences.get("adjusted_conjunctions", {})

    for h, h_data in geometry["houses"].items():
        lord = h_data["lord"]
        occupants = set(h_data.get("occupants", []))
        cusp_lon = float(h_data.get("cusp_longitude", 0.0))
        contributions: Dict[str, float] = {}
        total_signed = 0.0
        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            i_val = p2c[p][h]
            p_lon_val = float(d1_grahas[p].get("longitude", 0.0))
            p_sign = d1_grahas[p].get("sign") or ZODIAC_SIGNS[int(p_lon_val // 30) % 12]
            p_deg_in_sign = float(d1_grahas[p].get("degree_0_to_30", p_lon_val % 30.0))
            dist_to_cusp_val = _shortest_distance(p_lon_val, cusp_lon)

            # Aspect and conjunction specifics
            if p in asp_virupas_map and h in asp_virupas_map[p]:
                asp_virupas = float(asp_virupas_map[p][h])
            else:
                asp_virupas = float(aspects.get_graha_drishti(p, p_lon_val, cusp_lon))

            if p in asp_to_cusp_map and h in asp_to_cusp_map[p]:
                asp_pct = float(asp_to_cusp_map[p][h])
            else:
                asp_pct = (asp_virupas / 60.0) * 100.0

            raw_c_pct = float(raw_conj_map.get(p, {}).get(h, 0.0))
            adj_c_pct = float(adj_conj_map.get(p, {}).get(h, 0.0))
            eff_i_val = i_val

            if planets_eval and p in planets_eval and "dignity" in planets_eval[p]:
                d_p = float(planets_eval[p]["dignity"].get("unit_score", 0.0))
                d_status = str(planets_eval[p]["dignity"].get("status", ""))
            else:
                d_status = _get_dignity_status(d1_grahas[p])
                d_p = DICARA_D1_DIGNITY_MAP.get(d_status, 0.0)

            # House Lord vs Non-Lord on House Cusp (Phaladīpikā 15.1–15.5, 15.7)
            if p == lord:
                f_eff = 2.0
                lagna_f = float(func_natures[p]["score"]) if p in func_natures else 3.0
                inh_score = float(func_natures[p].get("rule_breakdown", {}).get("inherent_score", 0.0)) if p in func_natures else 0.0
                friend_mod = 0.0
                role = "Lord"
            else:
                lagna_f = float(func_natures[p]["score"])
                inh_score = float(func_natures[p].get("rule_breakdown", {}).get("inherent_score", 0.0))
                h_sign = h_data.get("sign")

                # Check if target house sign is planet P's Exaltation or Mūlatrikoṇa / Domicile sign
                # Restrict to non-malefics (inh_score >= 0.0 or lagna_f >= 0.0) so functional malefics
                # don't receive an artificial +0.50 friendship bonus when aspecting an enemy sign
                is_exalt_or_root = (
                    (
                        h_sign == DICARA_MOOLATRIKONA_SIGNS.get(p)
                        or h_sign == EXALTATION_SIGNS.get(p)
                        or SIGN_LORDS.get(h_sign) == p
                    )
                    and (inh_score >= 0.0 or lagna_f >= 0.0)
                )

                rel_p_to_l = get_natural_relationship(p, lord)
                rel_l_to_p = get_natural_relationship(lord, p)
                if is_exalt_or_root:
                    friend_mod = 0.50
                elif rel_p_to_l == "Friend" and rel_l_to_p == "Friend":
                    friend_mod = 0.50
                elif "Enemy" in (rel_p_to_l, rel_l_to_p):
                    friend_mod = -0.50
                elif "Friend" in (rel_p_to_l, rel_l_to_p):
                    friend_mod = 0.25
                else:
                    friend_mod = 0.0

                # Scale lagna_f by 0.50 so non-lords stay bounded on the [-2.0, +2.0] scale
                # below or equal to the House Lord (2.0), preserving Yogakāraka status and natural benefic cushions (15.1, 15.7):
                f_eff = 0.50 * lagna_f + 0.50 * inh_score + 0.35 * friend_mod
                role = "Occupant" if p in occupants else "Aspect"

            # Continuous scaling across functional spectrum (eliminates 0.15 deadband cliff)
            signed_impact = _compute_signed_ray_impact(eff_i_val, d_p, f_eff, slope=0.65)

            # Upacaya (3, 6, 11) Malefic Occupant Rule (Phaladīpikā 15.18 & Ch. 4):
            # Only converts when the planet is functioning as a malefic (f_eff < 0).
            # Benefic 1st Lords / Yogakārakas are NEVER slashed.
            # Dignity scales the resilience boost positively (exalted gives strong resilience; debilitated weak).
            is_upacaya_occ = False
            if p in occupants and p != lord and p in ["Sun", "Mars", "Saturn"] and h in [3, 6, 11] and f_eff < 0.0:
                pos_scaled = scale_by_dignity(eff_i_val, d_p, 1.0)
                signed_impact = pos_scaled * 0.35
                is_upacaya_occ = True

            pre_gate_impact = signed_impact

            # Condition-Gate Cusp Influences by Combustion & Bhāva Sandhi (Phaladīpikā 15.1 amūḍha-hīnāḥ)
            # Attenuate positive rays from planets afflicted by negative multi-varga dignity (arinīca-mūḍha)
            p_occ_house = 1
            if planets_eval and p in planets_eval and "house" in planets_eval[p]:
                p_occ_house = int(planets_eval[p]["house"])
            else:
