                p: {11: 100.0 if p in ["Sun", "Mars"] else 0.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
            }
        }

        # Case A: Sun (1st Lord) in H11 must NOT be slashed by 0.35x
        res_a = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences)
        sun_contrib = res_a[11]["planet_contributions"]["Sun"]
        # Sun: eff_i_val = 100, d_p = 0.8, f_eff = 0.50 * 3.0 + 0.35 * 0.25 = 1.5875 -> scaled = 180.0, f_mult = 1.0 + 0.65*0.5875 = 1.381875 -> 248.74
        assert sun_contrib > 200.0  # Full positive blessing, not slashed to ~60!
        assert pytest.approx(sun_contrib, abs=1.0) == 248.74

        # Case B: Mars (malefic f_eff < 0) dignity scaling in Upacaya
        # Exalted Mars (+1.0 dignity): pos_scaled = 100 * (1 + 1.0) = 200 -> 200 * 0.35 = 70.0
        d1_grahas["Mars"]["dignity"] = "Exalted"
        res_exalt = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences)
        assert res_exalt[11]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"] == pytest.approx(70.0, abs=0.5)

        # Neutral Mars (0.0 dignity): pos_scaled = 100 * 1.0 = 100 -> 100 * 0.35 = 35.0
        d1_grahas["Mars"]["dignity"] = "Neutral Sign"
        res_neutral = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences)
        assert res_neutral[11]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"] == pytest.approx(35.0, abs=0.5)

        # Debilitated Mars (-1.0 dignity): pos_scaled = 100 * (1 - 0.8) = 20 -> 20 * 0.35 = 7.0
        d1_grahas["Mars"]["dignity"] = "Debilitated"
        res_deb = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences)
        assert res_deb[11]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"] == pytest.approx(7.0, abs=0.5)

        # Strict ordering: Exalted > Neutral > Debilitated (dignity is never inverted)
        assert (
            res_exalt[11]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"]
            > res_neutral[11]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"]
            > res_deb[11]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"]
        )

    def test_adaptation2_no_double_counting_natural_temperament(self):
        """
        Adaptation 2:
        Verifies that Yogakāraka Mars (+3.0 for Leo) and Yogakāraka Saturn (+2.0 for Taurus)
        retain positive effective functional nature on house cusps, rather than having their
        Yogakāraka status erased to 0.00 or flipped to negative malefic.
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {
                1: {"lord": "Sun", "sign": "Leo", "occupants": []},
                7: {"lord": "Venus", "sign": "Taurus", "occupants": []}
            }
        }
        # Leo Lagna: Mars owns 4 & 9 (Rāja Yogakāraka, score = +3.0)
        func_natures = {
            "Mars": {"score": 3.0, "classification": "Benefic"},
            "Saturn": {"score": 2.0, "classification": "Benefic"}
        }
        for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            if p not in func_natures:
                func_natures[p] = {"score": 1.0, "classification": "Benefic"}

        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"dignity": "Own Sign", "longitude": 10.0, "sign": "Aries"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        influences = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {
                p: {1: 100.0 if p == "Mars" else 0.0, 7: 100.0 if p == "Saturn" else 0.0}
                for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
            }
        }

        res = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences)
        # Mars aspect on H1 (ruled by Sun, mutual friends -> friend_mod = +0.50):
        # f_eff = +3.0 + 0.50 = +3.50 (strongly positive, NOT wiped to 0.00!)
        mars_on_h1 = res[1]["planet_contributions"]["Mars"]
        assert mars_on_h1 > 200.0

        # Saturn aspect on H7 (ruled by Venus, mutual friends -> friend_mod = +0.50):
        # f_eff = +2.0 + 0.50 = +2.50 (strongly positive, NOT flipped to malefic -0.40!)
        saturn_on_h7 = res[7]["planet_contributions"]["Saturn"]
        assert saturn_on_h7 > 200.0

    def test_adaptation3_continuous_deadband_smooth_scaling(self):
        """
        Adaptation 3:
        Verifies that near f_eff = 0, signed_impact transitions smoothly without cliff edges
        or 107% sign inversions.
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {1: {"lord": "Jupiter", "sign": "Pisces", "occupants": []}}
        }
        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"dignity": "Own Sign", "longitude": 10.0, "sign": "Aries"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        influences = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {p: {1: 100.0 if p == "Mercury" else 0.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        }

        # Step through f_lagna near 0: from -0.20 to +0.20
        # Mercury in Own Sign (d_p = +0.80), Jupiter to Mercury is Neutral (friend_mod = 0.0)
        outputs = []
        scores = [-0.20, -0.15, -0.10, -0.05, 0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20]
        for sc in scores:
            fn = {p: {"score": sc if p == "Mercury" else 1.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
            res = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, fn, d1_grahas, influences)
            outputs.append(res[1]["planet_contributions"]["Mercury"])

        # Every successive step must be monotonically increasing with no sudden cliff jumps
        for i in range(len(outputs) - 1):
            assert outputs[i+1] > outputs[i]
            # Max step jump should be smooth and reasonable (< 35 points per 0.05 change)
            assert (outputs[i+1] - outputs[i]) < 35.0

    def test_adaptation4_multi_varga_dignity_rescuing_positive_rays(self):
        """
        Adaptation 4:
        Verifies that a planet with negative D1 dignity but positive composite dignity (rescued
        by D9 / Vargottama) is not hit by the negative dignity penalty (arinīca-mūḍha).
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {1: {"lord": "Mars", "sign": "Aries", "occupants": []}}
        }
        func_natures = {p: {"score": 2.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        # In D1, Jupiter is in Enemy's Sign (d_d1 = -0.40)
        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"dignity": "Enemy's Sign", "longitude": 10.0, "sign": "Aries"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        influences = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {p: {1: 100.0 if p == "Jupiter" else 0.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        }

        # Case A: Rescued in D9 to composite unit_score = +0.80 (Vargottama)
        planets_eval_rescued = {
            "Jupiter": {
                "dignity": {"unit_score": 0.80},
                "combustion": {"loss_ratio": 0.0},
                "bhava_sandhi": {"penalty_ratio": 0.0}
            }
        }
        res_rescued = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(
            geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences, planets_eval=planets_eval_rescued
        )
        jup_rescued = res_rescued[1]["planet_contributions"]["Jupiter"]

        # Case B: Negative composite dignity unit_score = -0.40 (not rescued)
        planets_eval_afflicted = {
            "Jupiter": {
                "dignity": {"unit_score": -0.40},
                "combustion": {"loss_ratio": 0.0},
                "bhava_sandhi": {"penalty_ratio": 0.0}
            }
        }
        res_afflicted = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(
            geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences, planets_eval=planets_eval_afflicted
        )
        jup_afflicted = res_afflicted[1]["planet_contributions"]["Jupiter"]

        # Rescued planet delivers much stronger ray than un-rescued planet
        assert jup_rescued > jup_afflicted * 2.0
        # Rescued planet is not penalized by condition_integrity
        assert jup_rescued > 180.0
        assert pytest.approx(jup_rescued, abs=1.0) == 200.47

    def test_adaptation5_late_early_cusp_sandhi_relief_trump_mars(self):
        """
        Adaptation 5:
        Verifies Donald Trump's Mars geometry:
        Ascendant cusp is at 29.97° Leo (0.03° from border).
        Mars is at 26.78° Leo (3.19° from cusp, inside the house facing the door).
        Mars must have 0.0 sandhi_penalty (relieved by cusp proximity per Phaladīpikā 15.14 & 8.34-35).
        """
        geometry = {
            "ascendant_sign": "Leo",
            "houses": {
                1: {"sign": "Leo", "lord": "Sun", "cusp_longitude": 149.97, "occupants": ["Mars"]}
            }
        }
        for h in range(2, 13):
            geometry["houses"][h] = {
                "sign": ZODIAC_SIGNS[(4 + h - 1) % 12],
                "lord": "Sun",
                "cusp_longitude": (4 + h - 1) * 30.0 + 29.97,
                "occupants": []
            }

        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"sign": "Aries", "longitude": 10.0, "degree_0_to_30": 10.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        d1_grahas["Mars"] = {"sign": "Leo", "longitude": 146.78, "degree_0_to_30": 26.78}
        func_natures = {p: {"score": 1.0, "classification": "Benefic"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        influences = {
            "planet_to_planet": {p1: {p2: 0.0 for p2 in CLASSICAL_PLANETS} for p1 in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
            "adjusted_conjunctions": {p: {h: 0.0 for h in range(1, 13)} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
            "raw_conjunctions": {p: {h: 0.0 for h in range(1, 13)} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
        }

        eval_res = evaluate_planets_hol(geometry, d1_grahas, func_natures, influences)
        mars_sandhi = eval_res["Mars"]["bhava_sandhi"]

        assert mars_sandhi["in_sandhi_zone"] is False
        assert mars_sandhi["penalty_ratio"] == 0.0
        assert mars_sandhi["retention_multiplier"] == 1.0

    def test_adaptation6_cusp_score_strict_monotonicity(self):
        """
        Adaptation 6:
        Verifies that Pillar 1 normalization (total_signed / 3.0 followed by 120 * tanh)
        is strictly monotonic:
        Adding an additional benefic ray strictly INCREASES the house score,
        and adding an additional malefic ray strictly DECREASES the house score.
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {1: {"lord": "Mars", "sign": "Aries", "occupants": []}}
        }
        func_natures = {
            "Jupiter": {"score": 2.0},
            "Venus": {"score": 2.0},
            "Sun": {"score": 2.0},
            "Moon": {"score": 2.0},
            "Saturn": {"score": -2.0},
        }
        for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            if p not in func_natures:
                func_natures[p] = {"score": 0.0}

        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"dignity": "Own Sign", "longitude": 10.0, "sign": "Aries"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }

        # Baseline: 3 strong benefic aspects
        inf_baseline = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {
                "Jupiter": {1: 90.0},
                "Venus": {1: 90.0},
                "Sun": {1: 90.0},
                "Moon": {1: 0.0},
                "Saturn": {1: 0.0},
                "Mars": {1: 0.0},
                "Mercury": {1: 0.0},
            }
        }
        res_baseline = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, inf_baseline)
        score_baseline = res_baseline[1]["score"]

        # Scenario 1: Add a 4th moderate benefic aspect (+20 ray from Moon)
        inf_plus_benefic = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {
                **inf_baseline["planet_to_cusp"],
                "Moon": {1: 45.0}  # Additional positive ray
            }
        }
        res_benefic = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, inf_plus_benefic)
        score_benefic = res_benefic[1]["score"]

        # Strict monotonicity: adding a benefic aspect MUST strictly increase the house score!
        assert score_benefic > score_baseline

        # Scenario 2: Add a malefic aspect from Saturn
        inf_plus_malefic = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {
                **inf_baseline["planet_to_cusp"],
                "Saturn": {1: 45.0}  # Negative ray
            }
        }
        res_malefic = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, inf_plus_malefic)
        score_malefic = res_malefic[1]["score"]

        # Strict monotonicity: adding a malefic aspect MUST strictly decrease the house score!
        assert score_malefic < score_baseline

    def test_bug_a_phase5_slightly_negative_functional_score_is_negative(self):
        """
        Bug A Fix: In evaluate_planets_hol(), when a planet Q has a slightly negative
        functional score in (-0.15, 0.0) such as f_q = -0.10 with bad dignity (d_q = -1.0),
        its ray onto planet P must be strictly negative, NOT flipped to a +200% positive ray.
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {h: {"sign": ZODIAC_SIGNS[h - 1], "lord": "Sun", "cusp_longitude": (h - 1) * 30.0 + 15.0, "occupants": []} for h in range(1, 13)}
        }
        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"sign": "Aries", "longitude": 10.0, "degree_0_to_30": 10.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        d1_grahas["Saturn"]["dignity"] = "Debilitated"  # d_q = -1.0

        func_natures = {p: {"score": 1.0, "classification": "Benefic"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        func_natures["Saturn"] = {"score": -0.10, "classification": "Malefic"}

        influences = {
            "planet_to_planet": {p1: {p2: 0.0 for p2 in CLASSICAL_PLANETS} for p1 in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
            "adjusted_conjunctions": {p: {h: 0.0 for h in range(1, 13)} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
            "raw_conjunctions": {p: {h: 0.0 for h in range(1, 13)} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
        }
        influences["planet_to_planet"]["Saturn"]["Sun"] = 50.0

        evals = evaluate_planets_hol(geometry, d1_grahas, func_natures, influences)
        incoming = evals["Sun"]["combined_influences"]["incoming_breakdown"]
        assert "Saturn" in incoming
        # Must be strictly negative, NOT flipped positive!
        assert incoming["Saturn"] < 0.0

    def test_bug_b_exalted_root_sign_positive_ray_trump_full_moon(self):
        """
        Bug B Fix: A Full Moon or natural benefic aspecting its own Exaltation or Mūlatrikoṇa
        sign (like Moon on Taurus H10 for Leo Lagna) must deliver a positive ray, rather than
        being turned into an enemy malefic.
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {
                10: {"lord": "Venus", "sign": "Taurus", "cusp_longitude": 54.4, "occupants": []}
            }
        }
        # Trump's Moon: Leo Lagna, Full Moon inherent = +1.0, rules H12 Cancer (-1.0) -> lagna_f = 0.0
        func_natures = {
            "Moon": {
                "score": 0.0,
                "classification": "Neutral",
                "rule_breakdown": {"inherent_score": 1.0, "trine_score": 0.0, "dusthana_score": -1.0}
            }
        }
        for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS:
            if p not in func_natures:
                func_natures[p] = {"score": 1.0, "rule_breakdown": {"inherent_score": 1.0}}

        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"dignity": "Friend's Sign", "longitude": 10.0, "sign": "Aries"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        d1_grahas["Moon"] = {"dignity": "Friend's Sign", "longitude": 261.2, "sign": "Sagittarius"}

        influences = {
            "planet_to_cusp": {p: {10: 0.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        }
        influences["planet_to_cusp"]["Moon"][10] = 29.2  # Trump's exact aspect onto H10 cusp

        res = evaluate_basic_house_conditions(geometry, func_natures, d1_grahas, influences)
        moon_contrib = res[10]["planet_contributions"]["Moon"]
        # Must be positive (Full Moon aspecting its own Exaltation/Mūlatrikoṇa sign Taurus)
        assert moon_contrib > 0.0

    def test_bug_c_three_planet_stellium_pairwise_cusp_dominance(self):
        """
        Bug C Fix: In a 3-planet stellium where Planet 1 is at 28°, Planet 2 is at 10°,
        and Planet 3 is at 9° near a 29° cusp:
        Planet 2 must steal from Planet 3 because they are 1° apart, even though
        Planet 1 is > 15° away from both of them.
        """
        geometry = {
            "houses": {
                1: {"sign": "Aries", "cusp_longitude": 29.0, "occupants": ["Mars", "Jupiter", "Venus"]}
            }
        }
        # Mars at 28°, Jupiter at 10°, Venus at 9°
        d1_grahas = {
            p: {"sign": "Pisces", "longitude": 345.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS
        }
        d1_grahas["Mars"] = {"sign": "Aries", "longitude": 28.0}
        d1_grahas["Jupiter"] = {"sign": "Aries", "longitude": 10.0}
        d1_grahas["Venus"] = {"sign": "Aries", "longitude": 9.0}

        adv_aspects = {"planets": {}}
        influences = calculate_cusp_and_planet_influences(geometry, d1_grahas, adv_aspects)
        adj_conj = influences["adjusted_conjunctions"]

        # Jupiter (10°) and Venus (9°) are 1° apart (< 15°).
        # Jupiter is closer to cusp (dist 19°) than Venus (dist 20°).
        # Therefore, Jupiter must steal from Venus:
        assert adj_conj["Jupiter"][1] > influences["raw_conjunctions"]["Jupiter"][1]
        assert adj_conj["Venus"][1] < influences["raw_conjunctions"]["Venus"][1]

    def test_minor_d_smooth_sandhi_relief_boundary(self):
        """
        Minor Item D: Verifies that cusp_relief smoothly ramps between 5° and 10°
        without any step discontinuity at 5.01°.
        """
        geometry = {
            "ascendant_sign": "Leo",
            "houses": {
                1: {"sign": "Leo", "lord": "Sun", "cusp_longitude": 140.0, "occupants": ["Mars"]}
            }
        }
        for h in range(2, 13):
            geometry["houses"][h] = {
                "sign": ZODIAC_SIGNS[(4 + h - 1) % 12], "lord": "Sun",
                "cusp_longitude": (4 + h - 1) * 30.0 + 20.0, "occupants": []
            }

        func_natures = {p: {"score": 1.0, "classification": "Benefic"} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        influences = {
            "planet_to_planet": {p1: {p2: 0.0 for p2 in CLASSICAL_PLANETS} for p1 in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
            "adjusted_conjunctions": {p: {h: 0.0 for h in range(1, 13)} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
            "raw_conjunctions": {p: {h: 0.0 for h in range(1, 13)} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS},
        }

        # Mars at 27.0° (dist_border = 3.0° -> raw_sandhi_pen = 0.25).
        # Test cusp distances: 4.9° (relief 0.0), 5.1° (relief ~0.02), 7.5° (relief 0.50), 10.1° (relief 1.0)
        # At 4.9° (cusp_lon = 147.0 - 4.9 = 142.1)
        geometry["houses"][1]["cusp_longitude"] = 142.1
        d1_grahas = {p: {"sign": "Aries", "longitude": 10.0, "degree_0_to_30": 10.0} for p in CLASSICAL_PLANETS}
        d1_grahas["Mars"] = {"sign": "Leo", "longitude": 147.0, "degree_0_to_30": 27.0}
        eval_4_9 = evaluate_planets_hol(geometry, d1_grahas, func_natures, influences)
        pen_4_9 = eval_4_9["Mars"]["bhava_sandhi"]["penalty_ratio"]

        # At 5.1° (cusp_lon = 147.0 - 5.1 = 141.9)
        geometry["houses"][1]["cusp_longitude"] = 141.9
        eval_5_1 = evaluate_planets_hol(geometry, d1_grahas, func_natures, influences)
        pen_5_1 = eval_5_1["Mars"]["bhava_sandhi"]["penalty_ratio"]

        # At 7.5° (cusp_lon = 147.0 - 7.5 = 139.5)
        geometry["houses"][1]["cusp_longitude"] = 139.5
        eval_7_5 = evaluate_planets_hol(geometry, d1_grahas, func_natures, influences)
        pen_7_5 = eval_7_5["Mars"]["bhava_sandhi"]["penalty_ratio"]

        # At 10.5° (cusp_lon = 147.0 - 10.5 = 136.5)
        geometry["houses"][1]["cusp_longitude"] = 136.5
        eval_10_5 = evaluate_planets_hol(geometry, d1_grahas, func_natures, influences)
        pen_10_5 = eval_10_5["Mars"]["bhava_sandhi"]["penalty_ratio"]

        # Penalty must be 0.0 at 4.9°, tiny (~0.005) at 5.1°, half (~0.125) at 7.5°, and full (0.25) at 10.5°
        assert pen_4_9 == 0.0
        assert 0.0 < pen_5_1 < 0.02
        assert pytest.approx(pen_7_5, abs=0.01) == 0.125
        assert pytest.approx(pen_10_5, abs=0.001) == 0.25

    def test_pillar1_calculation_ledger_schema_and_math_proof(self):
        """
        Step 4 Backend Test:
        Verifies that for every house H in 1..12, components['basic_house_condition']['calculation_ledger']
        exists, contains exactly 9 rows (7 classical planets + Rahu, Ketu in canonical order),
        and satisfies all mathematical proof equations down to the footer summary.
        """
        from app import app
        import math
        client = app.test_client()
        res = client.get('/api/chart/9bb691ba-c4fd-40f1-83eb-289b4e275ac8')
        assert res.status_code == 200
        hol = res.get_json()["data"]["houses_of_life"]

        expected_grahas = ["Sun", "Moon", "Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Venus", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu"]

        for h in range(1, 13):
            h_data = hol["houses"][str(h)]
            bhc = h_data["components"]["basic_house_condition"]
            assert "calculation_ledger" in bhc, f"House {h} missing calculation_ledger"
            ledger = bhc["calculation_ledger"]

            # Header metadata
            assert ledger["cusp_sign"] == h_data["sign"]
            assert ledger["lord"] == h_data["lord"]
            assert ledger["baseline_divisor"] == 4.0
            assert ledger["tanh_saturation_cap"] == 120.0

            rows = ledger["rows"]
            assert len(rows) == 9, f"House {h} ledger rows count expected 9, got {len(rows)}"
            assert [r["planet"] for r in rows] == expected_grahas

            # Verify row-level mathematical identity
            row_sum = 0.0
            for r in rows:
                if not r["is_node"]:
                    # base_ray_pct == max(aspect_pct, adj_conj_pct)
                    assert pytest.approx(r["base_ray_pct"], abs=0.02) == max(r["aspect_pct"], r["adj_conj_pct"])
                row_sum += r["net_contribution"]

            # Footer mathematical proofs
            assert pytest.approx(row_sum, abs=0.1) == ledger["raw_total_signed"]
            raw_div = round(ledger["raw_total_signed"] / 4.0, 2)
            assert pytest.approx(raw_div, abs=0.02) == ledger["raw_divided_score"]
            expected_final = round(120.0 * math.tanh(ledger["raw_divided_score"] / 120.0), 2)
            assert pytest.approx(expected_final, abs=0.02) == ledger["final_score"]
            assert ledger["final_score"] == bhc["score"]

import pytest
import urllib.request
from playwright.sync_api import Page, expect

FLASK_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:5001"
CHART_ID = "angelina-jolie"

def is_server_running(url=FLASK_URL):
    try:
        urllib.request.urlopen(url)
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False

@pytest.fixture(scope="module", autouse=True)
def check_server():
    if not is_server_running():
        pytest.skip("Flask server is not running on port 5001")

def init_page(page: Page):
    page.goto(FLASK_URL, wait_until="domcontentloaded")
    page.wait_for_timeout(800)
    page.evaluate(f"loadChart('{CHART_ID}')")
    page.wait_for_timeout(1200)

def test_houses_of_life_widget_renders(page: Page):
    init_page(page)
    page.evaluate("assignWidget('houses-of-life', document.querySelector('.grid-cell'))")

    assert page.locator("#widgetMaximizeModal").is_visible()

def test_houses_of_life_basic_sheet_renders(page: Page):
    """
    Step 4 Frontend UI Test:
    Clicking the Basic (30%) cell for House 1 opens the receipt inspector,
    renders the spreadsheet-grade Pillar 1 calculation ledger with 10 column headers,
    9 planet/node rows, and 3 footer proof rows, and verifies the 12px font floor.
    """
    init_page(page)
    page.evaluate("assignWidget('houses-of-life', document.querySelector('.grid-cell'))")
    page.wait_for_timeout(600)

    # Click Basic (30%) cell on House 1
    basic_cell_h1 = page.locator(".hol-house-row[data-house='1'] .hol-cell-basic")
    assert basic_cell_h1.count() > 0
    basic_cell_h1.click()
    page.wait_for_timeout(500)

    # Switch to receipt tab if on compact width
    tab_receipt = page.locator(".hol-tab-btn[data-view='receipt']")
    if tab_receipt.is_visible():
        tab_receipt.click()
        page.wait_for_timeout(300)

    # Verify spreadsheet table renders
    sheet_table = page.locator(".hol-basic-sheet-table")
    assert sheet_table.is_visible()

    # Verify 10 column headers
    headers = sheet_table.locator("thead th")
    assert headers.count() == 10
    header_texts = [headers.nth(i).inner_text().strip() for i in range(10)]
    assert "GRAHA" in header_texts[0].upper()
    assert "NET RAY" in header_texts[9].upper()

    # Verify exactly 9 rows (7 classical planets + Rahu + Ketu)
    rows = sheet_table.locator("tbody tr")
    assert rows.count() == 9

    # Verify 3 footer proof rows
    footer_rows = sheet_table.locator("tfoot tr")
    assert footer_rows.count() == 3
    assert "∑ Raw Signed Cusp Rays" in footer_rows.nth(0).inner_text()
    assert "÷ Fixed Baseline Divisor" in footer_rows.nth(1).inner_text()
    assert "Final Basic Score" in footer_rows.nth(2).inner_text()

    # Verify strict 12px font floor on headers and cells
    for i in range(headers.count()):
        font_size_px = float(headers.nth(i).evaluate("el => parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(el).fontSize)"))
        assert font_size_px >= 12.0, f"Header {i} font size {font_size_px}px is below 12px floor"

    # Test toggle expand button
    expand_btn = page.locator(".hol-btn-expand-basic")
    assert expand_btn.count() > 0
    expand_btn.click()
    page.wait_for_timeout(300)
    assert page.locator(".hol-receipt-right.hol-sheet-expanded").count() > 0
    expand_btn.click()
    page.wait_for_timeout(300)
    assert page.locator(".hol-receipt-right.hol-sheet-expanded").count() == 0

## 0. AI Interpretation Directive & Prompt Header
> **Role**: You are an enlightened, compassionate, and precise Vedic astrologer evaluating this natal horoscope.
> **Critical Technical Context**: This chart is calculated using **Ernst Wilhelm's Kala integrated paradigm**:
> 1. Signs are **Tropical** (the solstices define 0° Cancer/Capricorn; signs describe psychological seasons & physical vehicles).
> 2. House Cusps are **Campanus 3D** (the true physical horizon and prime vertical).
> 3. Nakshatras are **Sidereal Equatorial** (the cosmic background star field anchored to the Galactic Center).
> *Do not convert these Tropical placements back into sidereal signs; analyze the signs, houses, and nakshatras exactly as given below.*
>
> **Interpretation Protocol**:
> - **Core Horizon Vitality**: First analyze the Ascendant, its Lord (Lagneśa), and the Moon to evaluate mental temperament, biological vitality, and emotional baseline.
> - **Soul Purpose & Swāṃśa**: Interpret the Ātmakāraka (Soul significator) and its placement in D1 and D9 (Kārakāṃśa/Svāṃśa).
> - **Career & Worldly Karma**: Synthesize the 10th house, D10 Daśāṃśa, and Amātyakāraka (career intellect).

> - **Constructive Remediation**: Conclude with grounded, practical lifestyle guidance to harmonize challenged planets.
